#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>
#include <fstream>
#include <map>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <array>
#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>   // ORB������ȡ
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>

class LidarRingSaver {
public:
    LidarRingSaver(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : saved_(false) {
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        // ���Ŀ¼�����ھʹ���
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_ERROR("Failed to create directory: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }

        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarRingSaver::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        // if (saved_) {
        //     return; // ֻ�����һ֡
        // }
        


        ros::Time t1 = ros::Time::now();
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_intensity(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> iter_ring(*msg, "ring");

        // std::map<int, std::vector<std::array<float,4>>> ring_points;

        // std::string total_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points.txt";
        // std::ofstream total_ofs(total_file, std::ios::trunc);
        // if (!total_ofs.is_open()) {
        //     ROS_ERROR("Cannot open %s", total_file.c_str());
        //     return;
        // }

        int idx[NUM_RINGS] = {0};

        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end();
               ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_intensity, ++iter_ring) {
            int ring_id = *iter_ring;
            float x = *iter_x;
            float y = *iter_y;
            float z = *iter_z;
            float intensity = *iter_intensity;
            // float depth = std::sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);

            // ring_points[ring_id].push_back({x, y, z, intensity});
            // total_ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << " " << ring_id << "\n";

            if (ring_id < NUM_RINGS && idx[ring_id] < POINTS_PER_RING) {
                intensity_img.at<float>(ring_id, idx[ring_id]) = intensity;
                // depth_img.at<float>(ring_id, idx[ring_id]) = depth;
                idx[ring_id]++;
            }
        }
        ros::Time t2 = ros::Time::now();
        // total_ofs.close();
        double time = (t2 - t1).toSec() ; 
        std::cout<<"ǿ��ͼ��ȡ��ʱ��"<<time<<std::endl;
        // ���� ring_x.txt
        // for (auto& kv : ring_points) {
        //     int ring_id = kv.first;
        //     std::string filename = output_dir_ + "/ring_" + std::to_string(ring_id) + ".txt";
        //     std::ofstream ofs(filename, std::ios::trunc);
        //     if (!ofs.is_open()) {
        //         ROS_ERROR("Cannot open file %s", filename.c_str());
        //         continue;
        //     }
        //     for (auto& pt : kv.second) {
        //         ofs << pt[0] << " " << pt[1] << " " << pt[2] << " " << pt[3] << "\n";
        //     }
        //     ofs.close();
        // }

        // --- ����ͼ�� ---
        cv::Mat intensity_norm, depth_norm;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img, intensity_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        cv::normalize(depth_img, depth_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        // ����α��ɫͼ
        cv::Mat intensity_color, depth_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_norm, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        cv::applyColorMap(depth_norm, depth_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);

        // ����Ҷ�ͼ��α��ɫͼ
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_gray.png", intensity_norm);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_gray.png", depth_norm);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_colormap.png", intensity_color);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_colormap.png", depth_color);

        // ============ ORB ������ȡ ============
        ros::Time t3 = ros::Time::now();
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(100); // ��ȡ���1000��������
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;

        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_norm, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);
        ros::Time t4 = ros::Time::now();
        double time2 = (t4 - t3).toSec() ; 
        std::cout<<"������ȡ��ʱ��"<<time2<<std::endl;
        // ����������
        // cv::Mat orb_vis;
        // cv::drawKeypoints(intensity_norm, keypoints, orb_vis, cv::Scalar(0,255,0), cv::DrawMatchesFlags::DEFAULT);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb.png", orb_vis);

        // // ���������ӵ��ļ�
        // std::ofstream desc_file(output_dir_ + "/orb_descriptors.txt");
        // if (desc_file.is_open()) {
        //     for (int i = 0; i < descriptors.rows; i++) {
        //         for (int j = 0; j < descriptors.cols; j++) {
        //             desc_file << (int)descriptors.at<unsigned char>(i,j) << " ";
        //         }
        //         desc_file << "\n";
        //     }
        //     desc_file.close();
        // }

        // ROS_INFO("Saved all results + ORB features to folder: %s", output_dir_.c_str());

        // saved_ = true;
    }

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    bool saved_;
    std::string output_dir_;
        const int NUM_RINGS = 128;
        const int POINTS_PER_RING = 2500;

        cv::Mat intensity_img = cv::Mat::zeros(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F);
        cv::Mat depth_img     = cv::Mat::zeros(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F);
};

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_ring_saver");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~"); // ʹ��˽�������ռ䣬���ڼ��ز���

    LidarRingSaver saver(nh);

    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}



// fast_lidar_ring_saver.cpp
#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>

#include <fstream>
#include <map>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <array>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>

class LidarRingSaver {
public:
    LidarRingSaver(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : saved_(false) {
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_ERROR("Failed to create directory: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }
        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarRingSaver::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        if (saved_) return; // ֻ�����һ֡

        const int NUM_RINGS = 128;
        const int POINTS_PER_RING = 2500;


        // Ԥ����ͼ�� (float)
        cv::Mat intensity_img(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
        cv::Mat depth_img    (NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));

        // Ԥȡÿ�е� pointer ��������ѭ����Ƶ������ ptr/at��
        std::vector<float*> intensity_rows(NUM_RINGS);
        std::vector<float*> depth_rows(NUM_RINGS);
        
        auto t_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RINGS; ++r) {
            intensity_rows[r] = intensity_img.ptr<float>(r);
            depth_rows[r] = depth_img.ptr<float>(r);
        }

        // ��������ȡ PointCloud2 �ֶ�
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_intensity(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> iter_ring(*msg, "ring");

        // ���ڰ� ring ��㣨����ԭ�������
        // std::map<int, std::vector<std::array<float,4>>> ring_points;

        // �����ļ�
        // std::string total_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points.txt";
        // std::ofstream total_ofs(total_file, std::ios::trunc);
        // if (!total_ofs.is_open()) {
        //     ROS_ERROR("Cannot open %s", total_file.c_str());
        //     return;
        // }

        // ÿ�� ring �ĵ�ǰ��������������˳������У�
        std::vector<int> idx(NUM_RINGS, 0);

        // ��ѭ����ʹ�õ����������ȡ��д�������ڴ�
        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end();
               ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_intensity, ++iter_ring) {

            int ring_id = static_cast<int>(*iter_ring);
            if (ring_id < 0 || ring_id >= NUM_RINGS) continue;

            int col = idx[ring_id];
            if (col >= POINTS_PER_RING) {
                // ���ĳ�ߵ�������Ԥ�����������������ĵ㣨���账����
                continue;
            }

            float x = *iter_x;
            float y = *iter_y;
            float z = *iter_z;
            float intensity = *iter_intensity;
            // float depth = std::sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);

            // ֱ��д�������ڴ棨�����Ѻã�
            intensity_rows[ring_id][col] = intensity;
            // depth_rows[ring_id][col] = depth;

            // ����ԭʼ���ݽṹ�����㰴�������� txt
            // ring_points[ring_id].push_back({x, y, z, intensity});
            // total_ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << " " << ring_id << "\n";

            idx[ring_id]++; // Ϊ�� ring ������һ���к�
        }
        // total_ofs.close();

        auto t_map_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // ����ÿ���߶�Ӧ�� txt �ļ�
        // for (auto& kv : ring_points) {
        //     int ring_id = kv.first;
        //     std::string filename = output_dir_ + "/ring_" + std::to_string(ring_id) + ".txt";
        //     std::ofstream ofs(filename, std::ios::trunc);
        //     if (!ofs.is_open()) {
        //         ROS_ERROR("Cannot open file %s", filename.c_str());
        //         continue;
        //     }
        //     for (auto& pt : kv.second) {
        //         ofs << pt[0] << " " << pt[1] << " " << pt[2] << " " << pt[3] << "\n";
        //     }
        //     ofs.close();
        // }

        // ��һ��������ͼ����ԭ�߼�һ�£�
        cv::Mat intensity_norm, depth_norm;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img, intensity_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        // cv::normalize(depth_img, depth_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        cv::Mat intensity_color, depth_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_norm, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        // cv::applyColorMap(depth_norm, depth_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);

        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_gray.png", intensity_norm);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_gray.png", depth_norm);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_colormap.png", intensity_color);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_colormap.png", depth_color);
       
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(200); // ��ȡ��� 1000 ������
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        auto t_map_end1 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_norm, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);
        auto t_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        // ���ӻ��ؼ���
        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        cv::drawKeypoints(intensity_norm, keypoints, orb_vis, cv::Scalar(0,255,0),
                          cv::DrawMatchesFlags::DRAW_RICH_KEYPOINTS);
     
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb.png", orb_vis);

     

        double t_map_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_map_end - t_start).count();
        double t_save_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_end - t_map_end1).count();

        ROS_INFO("ӳ��ǿ��/���ͼ��ʱ: %.2f ms | �������һ����ʱ: %.2f ms", t_map_ms, t_save_ms);
        ROS_INFO("���������: %s", output_dir_.c_str());

        saved_ = true;
    }

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    bool saved_;
    std::string output_dir_;
};

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_ring_saver_fast");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");
    LidarRingSaver saver(nh);
    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}


# 0. Install dependencies
sudo apt update && sudo apt install -y libpcl-dev libpcap-dev libyaml-cpp-dev openssl

# 1. Navigate to source directory
cd HesaiLidar_SDK_2.0

# 2. Create build directory and navigate to build directory
mkdir -p build && cd build

# 3. Configure project with Cmake
#    - Add -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release for optimized compilation
cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..

# 4. Compile SDK
#    - Use -j$(nproc) to utilize all CPU cores
make -j$(nproc)

#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>
#include <fstream>
#include <map>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <array>
#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>   // ORB������ȡ
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>

class LidarRingSaver {
public:
    LidarRingSaver(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : saved_(false) ,count(0){
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        // ���Ŀ¼�����ھʹ���
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_ERROR("Failed to create directory: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }

        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarRingSaver::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        // if (saved_) {
        //     return; // ֻ�����һ֡
        // }
        if(count == 2)
        {
            return;
        }


        ros::Time t1 = ros::Time::now();
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_intensity(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> iter_ring(*msg, "ring");

        std::map<int, std::vector<std::array<float,4>>> ring_points;

        std::string total_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points.txt";
        std::ofstream total_ofs(total_file, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!total_ofs.is_open()) {
            ROS_ERROR("Cannot open %s", total_file.c_str());
            return;
        }

        int idx[NUM_RINGS] = {0};

        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end();
               ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_intensity, ++iter_ring) {
            int ring_id = *iter_ring;
            float x = *iter_x;
            float y = *iter_y;
            float z = *iter_z;
            float intensity = *iter_intensity;
            float depth = std::sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);

            ring_points[ring_id].push_back({x, y, z, intensity});
            total_ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << " " << ring_id << "\n";

            if (ring_id < NUM_RINGS && idx[ring_id] < POINTS_PER_RING) {
                intensity_img.at<float>(ring_id, idx[ring_id]) = intensity;
                depth_img.at<float>(ring_id, idx[ring_id]) = depth;
                idx[ring_id]++;
            }
        }
        ros::Time t2 = ros::Time::now();
        // total_ofs.close();
        double time = (t2 - t1).toSec() ; 
        // std::cout<<"ǿ��ͼ��ȡ��ʱ��"<<time<<std::endl;
        // ���� ring_x.txt
        for (auto& kv : ring_points) {
            int ring_id = kv.first;
            std::string filename = output_dir_ + "/ring_" + std::to_string(ring_id) + ".txt";
            std::ofstream ofs(filename, std::ios::trunc);
            if (!ofs.is_open()) {
                ROS_ERROR("Cannot open file %s", filename.c_str());
                continue;
            }
            for (auto& pt : kv.second) {
                ofs << pt[0] << " " << pt[1] << " " << pt[2] << " " << pt[3] << "\n";
            }
            ofs.close();
        }

        // --- ����ͼ�� ---
        cv::Mat intensity_norm, depth_norm;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img, intensity_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        cv::normalize(depth_img, depth_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        // ����α��ɫͼ
        cv::Mat intensity_color, depth_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_norm, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        cv::applyColorMap(depth_norm, depth_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);

        // ����Ҷ�ͼ��α��ɫͼ
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_gray.png", intensity_norm);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_gray.png", depth_norm);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_colormap.png", intensity_color);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_colormap.png", depth_color);

        // ============ ORB ������ȡ ============
        ros::Time t3 = ros::Time::now();
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(200); // ��ȡ���1000��������
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;

        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_norm, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);
        ros::Time t4 = ros::Time::now();
        double time2 = (t4 - t3).toSec() ; 
        // std::cout<<"������ȡ��ʱ��"<<time2<<std::endl;
        // ����������
        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        cv::drawKeypoints(intensity_norm, keypoints, orb_vis, cv::Scalar(0,255,0), cv::DrawMatchesFlags::DEFAULT);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb.png", orb_vis);

        // ���������ӵ��ļ�
        std::ofstream desc_file(output_dir_ + "/orb_descriptors.txt");
        if (desc_file.is_open()) {
            for (int i = 0; i < descriptors.rows; i++) {
                for (int j = 0; j < descriptors.cols; j++) {
                    desc_file << (int)descriptors.at<unsigned char>(i,j) << " ";
                }
                desc_file << "\n";
            }
            desc_file.close();
        }

        // ROS_INFO("Saved all results + ORB features to folder: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        count++;
        // saved_ = true;
    }

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    bool saved_;
    int count;
    std::string output_dir_;
        const int NUM_RINGS = 128;
        const int POINTS_PER_RING = 2500;

        cv::Mat intensity_img = cv::Mat::zeros(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F);
        cv::Mat depth_img     = cv::Mat::zeros(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F);
};

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_ring_saver");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~"); // ʹ��˽�������ռ䣬���ڼ��ز���

    LidarRingSaver saver(nh);

    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}








#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>

#include <fstream>
#include <map>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <array>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>

class LidarRingSaver {
public:
    LidarRingSaver(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : frame_count_(0) {
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_ERROR("Failed to create directory: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }
        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarRingSaver::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        if (frame_count_ >= 2) return;  // ֻ������֡

        const int NUM_RINGS = 128;
        const int POINTS_PER_RING = 2500;

        auto t_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        cv::Mat intensity_img(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
        cv::Mat depth_img    (NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));

        std::vector<float*> intensity_rows(NUM_RINGS);
        std::vector<float*> depth_rows(NUM_RINGS);
        for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RINGS; ++r) {
            intensity_rows[r] = intensity_img.ptr<float>(r);
            depth_rows[r] = depth_img.ptr<float>(r);
        }

        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_intensity(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> iter_ring(*msg, "ring");

        std::map<int, std::vector<std::array<float,4>>> ring_points;
        std::string total_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
        std::ofstream total_ofs(total_file, std::ios::trunc);

        std::vector<int> idx(NUM_RINGS, 0);

        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end();
               ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_intensity, ++iter_ring) {
            int ring_id = static_cast<int>(*iter_ring);
            if (ring_id < 0 || ring_id >= NUM_RINGS) continue;

            int col = idx[ring_id];
            if (col >= POINTS_PER_RING) continue;

            float x = *iter_x;
            float y = *iter_y;
            float z = *iter_z;
            float intensity = *iter_intensity;
            // float depth = std::sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);

            intensity_rows[ring_id][col] = intensity;
            // depth_rows[ring_id][col] = depth;

            ring_points[ring_id].push_back({x, y, z, intensity});
            total_ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << " " << ring_id << "\n";

            idx[ring_id]++;
        }
        total_ofs.close();

        // ����ÿ����
        for (auto& kv : ring_points) {
            int ring_id = kv.first;
            std::string filename = output_dir_ + "/ring_" + std::to_string(ring_id) + "_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
            std::ofstream ofs(filename, std::ios::trunc);
            for (auto& pt : kv.second) {
                ofs << pt[0] << " " << pt[1] << " " << pt[2] << " " << pt[3] << "\n";
            }
        }

        // ��һ��
        cv::Mat intensity_norm, depth_norm;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img, intensity_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        // cv::normalize(depth_img, depth_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        // α��ɫ
        cv::Mat intensity_color, depth_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_norm, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        // cv::applyColorMap(depth_norm, depth_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);

        // ����ͼ��
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_gray_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_norm);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_gray_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", depth_norm);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_color);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", depth_color);

        // ORB ����
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(50);
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_norm, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);

        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        cv::drawKeypoints(intensity_norm, keypoints, orb_vis, cv::Scalar(0,255,0),
                          cv::DrawMatchesFlags::DRAW_RICH_KEYPOINTS);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", orb_vis);

        ROS_INFO("֡ %d ������ɣ�������: %zu", frame_count_, keypoints.size());

        frame_count_++;
    }

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    int frame_count_;
    std::string output_dir_;
};

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_ring_saver_twoframes");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");

    LidarRingSaver saver(nh);

    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}



#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>
#include <chrono>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <fstream>
#include <map>

class LidarRangeImageSaver {
public:
    LidarRangeImageSaver(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : frame_count_(0) {
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_ERROR("Failed to create directory: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }

        // ⚠️ 禾赛 AT128 官方线束角度表应替换到这里
        vertical_angles_.resize(128);
        for (int i = 0; i < 128; i++) {
            // 示例：12.9° ~ -12.5° 均匀分布
            vertical_angles_[i] = (12.9 - i * (25.4 / 127.0)) * M_PI / 180.0;
        }

        min_el_ = *std::min_element(vertical_angles_.begin(), vertical_angles_.end());
        max_el_ = *std::max_element(vertical_angles_.begin(), vertical_angles_.end());

        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarRangeImageSaver::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        if (frame_count_ >= 2) return; // 只保存两帧

        auto t0 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        const int IMG_WIDTH = 2400; // 水平 120° * 0.05°
        const int IMG_HEIGHT = 128;

        cv::Mat intensity_img_u16(IMG_HEIGHT, IMG_WIDTH, CV_16U, cv::Scalar(0));
        cv::Mat depth_img_u16    (IMG_HEIGHT, IMG_WIDTH, CV_16U, cv::Scalar(0));

        // --- 打开输出文件 ---
        std::string total_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
        std::ofstream total_ofs(total_file, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!total_ofs.is_open()) {
            ROS_ERROR("Cannot open %s", total_file.c_str());
            return;
        }
        std::map<int, std::ofstream> ring_ofs;

        // --- 遍历点云 ---
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_intensity(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> iter_ring(*msg, "ring");

        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end();
               ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_intensity, ++iter_ring) {

            float x = *iter_x;
            float y = *iter_y;
            float z = *iter_z;
            float intensity = *iter_intensity;
            int ring_id = static_cast<int>(*iter_ring);

            if (ring_id < 0 || ring_id >= 128) continue;

            float depth = std::sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);

            // --- 写入总文件 ---
            total_ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << " " << ring_id << "\n";

            // --- 写入对应的 ring 文件 ---
            if (ring_ofs.find(ring_id) == ring_ofs.end()) {
                std::string fname = output_dir_ + "/ring_" + std::to_string(ring_id) +
                                    "_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
                ring_ofs[ring_id].open(fname, std::ios::trunc);
            }
            ring_ofs[ring_id] << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << "\n";

            // --- 转换到图像 ---
            float azimuth = atan2(y, x) * 180.0 / M_PI;
            if (azimuth < -60.0 || azimuth > 60.0) continue;

            int u = static_cast<int>((azimuth + 60.0) / 120.0 * IMG_WIDTH);
            float elevation = vertical_angles_[ring_id];
            int v = static_cast<int>((elevation - min_el_) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (IMG_HEIGHT - 1));

            if (u >= 0 && u < IMG_WIDTH && v >= 0 && v < IMG_HEIGHT) {
                intensity_img_u16.at<uint16_t>(IMG_HEIGHT - 1 - v, u) = static_cast<uint16_t>(intensity);
                depth_img_u16.at<uint16_t>(IMG_HEIGHT - 1 - v, u)     = static_cast<uint16_t>(depth * 100); // cm
            }
        }

        total_ofs.close();
        for (auto &kv : ring_ofs) kv.second.close();

        auto t1 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // --- 保存原始强度 & 深度 ---
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_raw_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_img_u16);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_raw_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", depth_img_u16);

        // --- 伪彩色可视化 ---
        cv::Mat intensity_norm, depth_norm;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img_u16, intensity_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        cv::normalize(depth_img_u16, depth_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        cv::Mat intensity_color, depth_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_norm, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        cv::applyColorMap(depth_norm, depth_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);

        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_color);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", depth_color);

        // --- ORB 特征提取（直接基于原始强度） ---
        cv::Mat intensity_for_orb;
        intensity_img_u16.convertTo(intensity_for_orb, CV_8U, 1.0); // 只做类型转换

        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(1000);
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_for_orb, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);

        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        cv::drawKeypoints(intensity_for_orb, keypoints, orb_vis, cv::Scalar(0,255,0),
                          cv::DrawMatchesFlags::DRAW_RICH_KEYPOINTS);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", orb_vis);

        auto t2 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        ROS_INFO("帧 %d 保存完成: 投影耗时 %.2f ms | 图像处理耗时 %.2f ms | ORB 特征数 %zu",
                 frame_count_,
                 std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(t1-t0).count(),
                 std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(t2-t1).count(),
                 keypoints.size());

        frame_count_++;
    }

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    int frame_count_;
    std::string output_dir_;
    std::vector<float> vertical_angles_; // rad
    float min_el_, max_el_;
};

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_range_image_saver");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");

    LidarRangeImageSaver saver(nh);
    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}



#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>

#include <fstream>
#include <map>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <array>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>

class LidarRingSaver {
public:
    LidarRingSaver(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : frame_count_(0) {
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_ERROR("Failed to create directory: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }
        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarRingSaver::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        if (frame_count_ >= 2) return;  // 只保存两帧

        const int NUM_RINGS = 128;
        const int POINTS_PER_RING = 2500;

        auto t_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        cv::Mat intensity_img(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
        cv::Mat depth_img    (NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));

        std::vector<float*> intensity_rows(NUM_RINGS);
        std::vector<float*> depth_rows(NUM_RINGS);
        for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RINGS; ++r) {
            intensity_rows[r] = intensity_img.ptr<float>(r);
            depth_rows[r] = depth_img.ptr<float>(r);
        }

        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_intensity(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> iter_ring(*msg, "ring");

        std::map<int, std::vector<std::array<float,4>>> ring_points;
        std::string total_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
        std::ofstream total_ofs(total_file, std::ios::trunc);

        std::vector<int> idx(NUM_RINGS, 0);

        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end();
               ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_intensity, ++iter_ring) {
            int ring_id = static_cast<int>(*iter_ring);
            if (ring_id < 0 || ring_id >= NUM_RINGS) continue;

            int col = idx[ring_id];
            if (col >= POINTS_PER_RING) continue;

            float x = *iter_x;
            float y = *iter_y;
            float z = *iter_z;
            float intensity = *iter_intensity;
            // float depth = std::sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);

            intensity_rows[ring_id][col] = intensity;
            // depth_rows[ring_id][col] = depth;

            ring_points[ring_id].push_back({x, y, z, intensity});
            total_ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << " " << ring_id << "\n";

            idx[ring_id]++;
        }
        total_ofs.close();

        // 保存每条线
        for (auto& kv : ring_points) {
            int ring_id = kv.first;
            std::string filename = output_dir_ + "/ring_" + std::to_string(ring_id) + "_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
            std::ofstream ofs(filename, std::ios::trunc);
            for (auto& pt : kv.second) {
                ofs << pt[0] << " " << pt[1] << " " << pt[2] << " " << pt[3] << "\n";
            }
        }

        // 归一化
        cv::Mat intensity_norm, depth_norm;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img, intensity_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        // cv::normalize(depth_img, depth_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        // 伪彩色
        cv::Mat intensity_color, depth_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_norm, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        // cv::applyColorMap(depth_norm, depth_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);

        // 保存图像
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_gray_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_norm);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_gray_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", depth_norm);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_color);
        // cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/depth_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", depth_color);

        // ORB 特征
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(200);
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_norm, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);

        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        cv::drawKeypoints(intensity_norm, keypoints, orb_vis, cv::Scalar(0,255,0),
                          cv::DrawMatchesFlags::DRAW_RICH_KEYPOINTS);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", orb_vis);

        ROS_INFO("帧 %d 保存完成，特征数: %zu", frame_count_, keypoints.size());

        frame_count_++;
    }

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    int frame_count_;
    std::string output_dir_;
};

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_ring_saver_twoframes");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");

    LidarRingSaver saver(nh);

    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}



#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>

#include <fstream>
#include <map>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <array>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>

class LidarRingSaver {
public:
    LidarRingSaver(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : frame_count_(0) {
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        nh.param<int>("sample_step", sample_step_, 1);  // 新增：采样步长，默认 5

        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_ERROR("Failed to create directory: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }
        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/velodyne_points", 1, &LidarRingSaver::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        if (frame_count_ >= 2) return;  // 只保存两帧

        const int NUM_RINGS = 16;
        const int POINTS_PER_RING = 1024 / sample_step_;  // 采样后每条线点数减少

        auto t_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        cv::Mat intensity_img(NUM_RINGS, POINTS_PER_RING, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));

        std::vector<float*> intensity_rows(NUM_RINGS);
        for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RINGS; ++r) {
            intensity_rows[r] = intensity_img.ptr<float>(r);
        }

        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_intensity(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> iter_ring(*msg, "ring");

        std::map<int, std::vector<std::array<float,4>>> ring_points;
        std::string total_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
        std::ofstream total_ofs(total_file, std::ios::trunc);

        std::vector<int> idx(NUM_RINGS, 0);
        std::vector<int> counter(NUM_RINGS, 0);  // 新增：采样计数器

        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end();
               ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_intensity, ++iter_ring) {
            int ring_id = static_cast<int>(*iter_ring);
            if (ring_id < 0 || ring_id >= NUM_RINGS) continue;

            // 采样：每 sample_step 个点取 1 个
            if (counter[ring_id] % sample_step_ != 0) {
                counter[ring_id]++;
                continue;
            }
            counter[ring_id]++;

            int col = idx[ring_id];
            if (col >= POINTS_PER_RING) continue;

            float x = *iter_x;
            float y = *iter_y;
            float z = *iter_z;
            float intensity = *iter_intensity;

            intensity_rows[ring_id][col] = intensity;

            ring_points[ring_id].push_back({x, y, z, intensity});
            total_ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity << " " << ring_id << "\n";

            idx[ring_id]++;
        }
        total_ofs.close();

        // 保存每条线
        for (auto& kv : ring_points) {
            int ring_id = kv.first;
            std::string filename = output_dir_ + "/ring_" + std::to_string(ring_id) + "_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
            std::ofstream ofs(filename, std::ios::trunc);
            for (auto& pt : kv.second) {
                ofs << pt[0] << " " << pt[1] << " " << pt[2] << " " << pt[3] << "\n";
            }
        }

        // 归一化
        cv::Mat intensity_norm;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img, intensity_norm, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        // 伪彩色
        cv::Mat intensity_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_norm, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);

        // 保存图像
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_gray_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_norm);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_color);

        // ORB 特征
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(50);
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_norm, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);

        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        cv::drawKeypoints(intensity_norm, keypoints, orb_vis, cv::Scalar(0,255,0),
                          cv::DrawMatchesFlags::DRAW_RICH_KEYPOINTS);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", orb_vis);

        ROS_INFO("帧 %d 保存完成，特征数: %zu", frame_count_, keypoints.size());

        frame_count_++;
    }

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    int frame_count_;
    int sample_step_;  // 采样步长
    std::string output_dir_;
};

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_ring_saver_twoframes");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");

    LidarRingSaver saver(nh);

    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}





#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>

#include <fstream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>
#include <chrono>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>

// === 自定义 clamp（兼容 C++11） ===
template <typename T>
inline T clampValue(T v, T lo, T hi)
{
    return (v < lo) ? lo : (v > hi) ? hi
                                    : v;
}

class LidarAngleFusion
{
public:
    LidarAngleFusion(ros::NodeHandle &nh) : frame_count_(0)
    {
        nh.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_output");
        nh.param<int>("sample_step", sample_step_, 1);
        nh.param<double>("h_fov", h_fov_deg_, 120.0);
        nh.param<int>("v_res", v_res_, 128);
        nh.param<std::string>("fusion_mode", fusion_mode_, std::string("max"));
        nh.param<std::string>("contrast_mode", contrast_mode_, std::string("none")); // 新增参数

        // 固定垂直角范围（禾赛 AT128）
        min_el_ = -12.5 * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_el_ = 12.9 * M_PI / 180.0;
        v_fov_deg_ = (max_el_ - min_el_) * 180.0 / M_PI;

        v_res_rad_ = max_el_ - min_el_;
        h_res_rad_ = h_fov_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;
        ang_res_rad_ = v_res_rad_ / static_cast<double>(v_res_);
        h_res_count_ = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::round(h_res_rad_ / ang_res_rad_)));

        min_az_ = -h_fov_deg_ / 2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_az_ = h_fov_deg_ / 2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;

        ROS_INFO("垂直角范围: %.2f° ~ %.2f° (%.2f°)，分辨率 %.3f°/pix",
                 min_el_ * 180 / M_PI, max_el_ * 180 / M_PI, v_fov_deg_,
                 ang_res_rad_ * 180 / M_PI);
        ROS_INFO("图像大小: %d × %d, 融合=%s, 对比度增强=%s",
                 v_res_, h_res_count_, fusion_mode_.c_str(), contrast_mode_.c_str());

        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST)
            ROS_WARN("mkdir failed or already exists: %s", output_dir_.c_str());

        sub_ = nh.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarAngleFusion::callback, this);
    }

    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg)
    {
        // if (frame_count_ >= 500)
        //     return;

        auto t0 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        cv::Mat intensity_sum(v_res_, h_res_count_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0));
        cv::Mat intensity_count(v_res_, h_res_count_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0));
        cv::Mat intensity_max(v_res_, h_res_count_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0));
        cv::Mat depth_for_max(v_res_, h_res_count_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0));

        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_int(*msg, "intensity");

        // std::string txt_file = output_dir_ + "/lidar_points_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".txt";
        // std::ofstream ofs(txt_file, std::ios::trunc);

        int idx = 0;
        for (; it_x != it_x.end(); ++it_x, ++it_y, ++it_z, ++it_int)
        {
            if (idx++ % sample_step_ != 0)
                continue;

            float x = *it_x, y = *it_y, z = *it_z;
            float intensity = *it_int*2;
            float depth = std::sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z);

            float azimuth = atan2(y, x);
            float elevation = atan2(z, std::sqrt(x * x + y * y));

            if (azimuth < min_az_ || azimuth > max_az_ || elevation < min_el_ || elevation > max_el_)
                continue;

            int u = static_cast<int>(std::round((max_az_ - azimuth) / (max_az_ - min_az_) * (h_res_count_ - 1)));
            int v = static_cast<int>(std::round((elevation - min_el_) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (v_res_ - 1)));
            v = v_res_ - 1 - v;
            u = clampValue(u, 0, h_res_count_ - 1);
            v = clampValue(v, 0, v_res_ - 1);

            intensity_sum.at<float>(v, u) += intensity;
            intensity_count.at<float>(v, u) += 1.0f;
            if (intensity > intensity_max.at<float>(v, u))
            {
                intensity_max.at<float>(v, u) = intensity;
                depth_for_max.at<float>(v, u) = depth;
            }

            // ofs << x << " " << y << " " << z << " " << intensity
            //     << " " << azimuth*180/M_PI << " " << elevation*180/M_PI << "\n";
        }
        // ofs.close();

        // --- 强度融合 ---
        bool use_max = (fusion_mode_ == "max" || fusion_mode_ == "MAX");
        cv::Mat intensity(v_res_, h_res_count_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0));
        for (int r = 0; r < v_res_; ++r)
            for (int c = 0; c < h_res_count_; ++c)
            {
                float cnt = intensity_count.at<float>(r, c);
                if (cnt > 0)
                    intensity.at<float>(r, c) = use_max ? intensity_max.at<float>(r, c)
                                                        : intensity_sum.at<float>(r, c) / cnt;
            }

        auto t1 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // --- 归一化 + 直方图增强 ---
        cv::Mat mask(v_res_, h_res_count_, CV_8U, cv::Scalar(0));
        for (int r = 0; r < v_res_; ++r)
            for (int c = 0; c < h_res_count_; ++c)
                if (intensity_count.at<float>(r, c) <= 0)
                    mask.at<uchar>(r, c) = 255;

        cv::Mat intensity_8u;
        cv::normalize(intensity, intensity_8u, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        // --- 对比度增强 ---
        cv::Mat intensity_enhanced;
        if (contrast_mode_ == "equalize")
        {
            cv::equalizeHist(intensity_8u, intensity_enhanced);
            ROS_INFO("使用全局直方图均衡化增强对比度");
        }
        else if (contrast_mode_ == "clahe")
        {
            cv::Ptr<cv::CLAHE> clahe = cv::createCLAHE(3.0, cv::Size(8, 8));
            clahe->apply(intensity_8u, intensity_enhanced);
            ROS_INFO("使用 CLAHE 增强局部对比度");
        }
        else
        {
            intensity_enhanced = intensity_8u.clone();
        }

        // --- 空洞填充 ---
        cv::Mat intensity_filled;
        intensity_filled = intensity_enhanced;
        // cv::inpaint(intensity_enhanced, mask, intensity_filled, 3.0, cv::INPAINT_TELEA);

        // --- 保存结果 ---
        cv::Mat intensity_color;
        cv::applyColorMap(intensity_filled, intensity_color, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        std::string base = output_dir_ + "/";
        cv::imwrite(base + "intensity_gray_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_filled);
        // cv::imwrite(base + "intensity_colormap_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", intensity_color);

        // --- ORB 特征 ---
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(100);
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_filled, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);

        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        if (intensity_filled.channels() == 1)
        {
            // 灰度图转BGR，便于彩色绘制
            cv::cvtColor(intensity_filled, orb_vis, cv::COLOR_GRAY2BGR);
        }
        else
        {
            orb_vis = intensity_filled.clone();
        }

        // 绘制小圆圈
        for (const auto &kp : keypoints)
        {
            int radius = std::max(1, (int)std::round(kp.size * 0.1)); // 🔹 半径更小
            cv::circle(orb_vis, kp.pt, radius, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), 1, cv::LINE_AA);
        }

        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/intensity_orb_frame" + std::to_string(frame_count_) + ".png", orb_vis);

        ROS_INFO("帧 %d 保存完成，特征数: %zu", frame_count_, keypoints.size());

        frame_count_++;
    }

    // auto t2 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    // ROS_INFO("帧 %d: 投影 %.1f ms | 归一化+增强 %.1f ms | ORB %.1f ms | 特征 %zu",
    //          frame_count_,
    //          std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(t1-t0).count(),
    //          std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(t2-t1).count(),
    //          std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(t2-t1).count(),
    //          keypoints.size());

    // frame_count_++;

private:
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    int frame_count_;
    int sample_step_;
    std::string output_dir_;
    std::string fusion_mode_;
    std::string contrast_mode_;

    double h_fov_deg_, v_fov_deg_;
    double min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_;
    int v_res_, h_res_count_;
    double v_res_rad_, h_res_rad_, ang_res_rad_;
};

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_angle_fusion_equalized");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");
    LidarAngleFusion node(nh);
    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}








// lidar_intensity_orb_match_with_Tguess.cpp
// 功能：角度投影生成强度图（v_res x h_res）-> CLAHE+Blur -> ORB -> knn+ratio+dist+2D-RANSAC
//       -> 将 2D 内点转成 3D-3D 对 (基于 pixel->3D 映射) -> 用 3D-RANSAC+SVD 求刚体变换 T_guess
//
// 参数（私有命名空间 ~）可用 rosparam 设置，见代码注释
//
// 输出：保存 intensity/color/match 可视化图像，以及 T_guess_frame_<i-1>_<i>.txt
//

#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>

#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>
#include <chrono>
#include <random>
#include <fstream>
#include <limits>

template<typename T>
inline T clampValue(T v, T lo, T hi) {
    return (v < lo) ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v);
}

class LidarIntensityORBMatchT {
public:
    LidarIntensityORBMatchT(ros::NodeHandle& nh) : nh_(nh), frame_idx_(0), has_prev_frame_(false) {
        // params (private)
        nh_.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, std::string("./lidar_output"));
        nh_.param<int>("sample_step", sample_step_, 1);
        nh_.param<std::string>("fusion_mode", fusion_mode_, std::string("max")); // "max" or "avg"
        nh_.param<int>("v_res", v_res_, 128);
        nh_.param<int>("h_res", h_res_, 500);
        nh_.param<double>("h_fov_deg", h_fov_deg_, 120.0);
        nh_.param<double>("v_min_deg", v_min_deg_, -12.5);
        nh_.param<double>("v_max_deg", v_max_deg_, 12.9);

        nh_.param<std::string>("contrast_mode", contrast_mode_, std::string("clahe")); // "clahe" or "equalize" or "none"
        nh_.param<bool>("enable_blur", enable_blur_, true);
        nh_.param<bool>("enable_inpaint", enable_inpaint_, true);

        // ORB params
        nh_.param<int>("orb_nfeatures", orb_nfeatures_, 1500);
        nh_.param<double>("orb_scaleFactor", orb_scaleFactor_, 1.1);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_nlevels", orb_nlevels_, 4);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_edgeThreshold", orb_edgeThreshold_, 5);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_patchSize", orb_patchSize_, 15);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_fastThreshold", orb_fastThreshold_, 5);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_wta_k", orb_wta_k_, 2);
        int orb_score_type_int = 0; // 0:HARRIS,1:FAST
        nh_.param<int>("orb_score_type", orb_score_type_int, 0);
        orb_score_type_ = (orb_score_type_int == 1) ? cv::ORB::FAST_SCORE : cv::ORB::HARRIS_SCORE;

        // Matching & RANSAC params
        nh_.param<float>("ratio_thresh", ratio_thresh_, 0.75f);
        nh_.param<int>("hamming_thresh", hamming_thresh_, 50);
        nh_.param<double>("ransac_2d_reproj", ransac_2d_reproj_, 3.0); // px
        nh_.param<double>("ransac_3d_thresh", ransac_3d_thresh_, 0.2); // meters
        nh_.param<int>("ransac_3d_iters", ransac_3d_iters_, 500);

        // Misc
        nh_.param<int>("save_match_vis_every", save_match_vis_every_, 1); // 1: save every frame
        nh_.param<int>("orb_vis_radius", orb_vis_radius_, 2);

        // ensure output dir
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_WARN("mkdir failed or already exists: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }

        // compute angle ranges (radian)
        min_az_ = -h_fov_deg_/2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_az_ =  h_fov_deg_/2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        min_el_ = v_min_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_el_ = v_max_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;

        ROS_INFO("Node started. Output dir: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        ROS_INFO("Image size: %d x %d (v: %.2f..%.2f deg, h_fov: %.2f deg)",
                 v_res_, h_res_, v_min_deg_, v_max_deg_, h_fov_deg_);

        sub_ = nh_.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarIntensityORBMatchT::callback, this);

        // rng
        rng_.seed(std::random_device{}());
    }

private:
    // ----- members -----
    ros::NodeHandle nh_;
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    std::string output_dir_;

    int sample_step_;
    std::string fusion_mode_;
    int v_res_, h_res_;
    double h_fov_deg_, v_min_deg_, v_max_deg_;
    double min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_;

    std::string contrast_mode_;
    bool enable_blur_, enable_inpaint_;

    // ORB
    int orb_nfeatures_, orb_nlevels_, orb_edgeThreshold_, orb_patchSize_, orb_fastThreshold_, orb_wta_k_;
    double orb_scaleFactor_;
    cv::ORB::ScoreType orb_score_type_;

    // matching params
    float ratio_thresh_;
    int hamming_thresh_;
    double ransac_2d_reproj_;
    double ransac_3d_thresh_;
    int ransac_3d_iters_;

    int save_match_vis_every_;
    int orb_vis_radius_;

    int frame_idx_;
    bool has_prev_frame_;

    // previous frame storage
    cv::Mat prev_img_;                 // prev intensity 8U
    cv::Mat prev_img_f_;               // prev float intensity (if needed)
    cv::Mat prev_desc_;
    std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> prev_kp_;
    std::vector<float> prev_pixel_x_, prev_pixel_y_, prev_pixel_z_; // size v_res_*h_res_, stores representative 3D point (max intensity)
    std::vector<char> prev_has_point_; // flags

    std::mt19937 rng_;

    struct PixAcc {
        float sum = 0.0f;
        int count = 0;
        float max_val = 0.0f;
        float max_x = 0.0f, max_y = 0.0f, max_z = 0.0f;
        float sum_x = 0.0f, sum_y = 0.0f, sum_z = 0.0f;
    };

    // ----- helper: compute rigid transform (SVD / Umeyama) -----
    // Input: vectors of corresponding 3D points P (source) and Q (target), same size >=1
    // Output: R (3x3), t (3x1). Returns true if succeeded.
    bool estimateRigidSVD(const std::vector<cv::Point3f>& P, const std::vector<cv::Point3f>& Q,
                          cv::Mat& R, cv::Mat& t) {
        size_t n = P.size();
        if (n == 0) return false;

        // compute centroids
        cv::Point3d meanP(0,0,0), meanQ(0,0,0);
        for (size_t i=0;i<n;i++) {
            meanP += cv::Point3d(P[i].x, P[i].y, P[i].z);
            meanQ += cv::Point3d(Q[i].x, Q[i].y, Q[i].z);
        }
        meanP *= (1.0 / (double)n);
        meanQ *= (1.0 / (double)n);

        // compute cross-covariance matrix H = sum (P_i - meanP) * (Q_i - meanQ)^T
        cv::Mat H = cv::Mat::zeros(3,3,CV_64F);
        for (size_t i=0;i<n;i++) {
            cv::Mat p = (cv::Mat_<double>(3,1) << P[i].x - meanP.x, P[i].y - meanP.y, P[i].z - meanP.z);
            cv::Mat q = (cv::Mat_<double>(1,3) << Q[i].x - meanQ.x, Q[i].y - meanQ.y, Q[i].z - meanQ.z);
            H += p * q;
        }

        // SVD
        cv::Mat U, S, Vt;
        cv::SVD::compute(H, S, U, Vt);
        R = Vt.t() * U.t();

        // ensure proper rotation (determinant = +1)
        double det = cv::determinant(R);
        if (det < 0) {
            // reflect
            cv::Mat B = cv::Mat::eye(3,3,CV_64F);
            B.at<double>(2,2) = -1;
            R = Vt.t() * B * U.t();
        }

        // translation
        cv::Mat meanP_m = (cv::Mat_<double>(3,1) << meanP.x, meanP.y, meanP.z);
        cv::Mat meanQ_m = (cv::Mat_<double>(3,1) << meanQ.x, meanQ.y, meanQ.z);
        t = meanQ_m - R * meanP_m;
        return true;
    }

    // RANSAC for 3D-3D correspondences. Returns best inlier mask and fills best R,t
    void ransac3D(const std::vector<cv::Point3f>& P_all, const std::vector<cv::Point3f>& Q_all,
                  double thresh, int max_iters,
                  cv::Mat& bestR, cv::Mat& bestt, std::vector<char>& best_inliers) {
        best_inliers.assign(P_all.size(), 0);
        size_t N = P_all.size();
        if (N < 3) {
            // not enough
            return;
        }

        std::uniform_int_distribution<size_t> uid(0, N-1);
        size_t best_count = 0;

        for (int it = 0; it < max_iters; ++it) {
            // sample 3 unique indices
            size_t i1 = uid(rng_), i2 = uid(rng_), i3 = uid(rng_);
            if (i2 == i1) continue;
            if (i3 == i1 || i3 == i2) continue;

            std::vector<cv::Point3f> Ps = {P_all[i1], P_all[i2], P_all[i3]};
            std::vector<cv::Point3f> Qs = {Q_all[i1], Q_all[i2], Q_all[i3]};

            cv::Mat R, t;
            if (!estimateRigidSVD(Ps, Qs, R, t)) continue;

            // count inliers
            size_t cnt = 0;
            for (size_t k=0;k<N;k++) {
                cv::Mat p = (cv::Mat_<double>(3,1) << P_all[k].x, P_all[k].y, P_all[k].z);
                cv::Mat p_trans = R * p + t;
                double dx = p_trans.at<double>(0,0) - Q_all[k].x;
                double dy = p_trans.at<double>(1,0) - Q_all[k].y;
                double dz = p_trans.at<double>(2,0) - Q_all[k].z;
                double dist = std::sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);
                if (dist <= thresh) cnt++;
            }

            if (cnt > best_count) {
                best_count = cnt;
                // mark inliers
                std::vector<char> mask(N, 0);
                for (size_t k=0;k<N;k++) {
                    cv::Mat p = (cv::Mat_<double>(3,1) << P_all[k].x, P_all[k].y, P_all[k].z);
                    cv::Mat p_trans = R * p + t;
                    double dx = p_trans.at<double>(0,0) - Q_all[k].x;
                    double dy = p_trans.at<double>(1,0) - Q_all[k].y;
                    double dz = p_trans.at<double>(2,0) - Q_all[k].z;
                    double dist = std::sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);
                    if (dist <= thresh) mask[k] = 1;
                }
                best_inliers = mask;
                bestR = R.clone();
                bestt = t.clone();
            }
        }

        // refine using all inliers if any
        size_t inlier_cnt = 0;
        for (auto v : best_inliers) if (v) inlier_cnt++;
        if (inlier_cnt >= 3) {
            std::vector<cv::Point3f> P_in, Q_in;
            for (size_t k=0;k<N;k++) if (best_inliers[k]) {
                P_in.push_back(P_all[k]);
                Q_in.push_back(Q_all[k]);
            }
            estimateRigidSVD(P_in, Q_in, bestR, bestt);
        }
    }

    // main callback
    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        if (!msg || msg->data.empty()) {
            ROS_WARN("Empty PointCloud2");
            return;
        }

        auto t0 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // accumulation grid
        std::vector<PixAcc> acc((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_);
        std::vector<char> has_point((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0);

        // iterate
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_int(*msg, "intensity");

        int idx_in = 0;
        for (; it_x != it_x.end(); ++it_x, ++it_y, ++it_z, ++it_int, ++idx_in) {
            if (sample_step_ > 1 && (idx_in % sample_step_ != 0)) continue;

            float x = *it_x, y = *it_y, z = *it_z, intensity = *it_int;
            if (!std::isfinite(x) || !std::isfinite(y) || !std::isfinite(z) || !std::isfinite(intensity)) continue;

            double az = std::atan2((double)y, (double)x);
            double el = std::atan2((double)z, std::sqrt((double)x*(double)x + (double)y*(double)y));

            if (az < min_az_ || az > max_az_) continue;
            if (el < min_el_ || el > max_el_) continue;

            double u_f = (az - min_az_) / (max_az_ - min_az_) * (h_res_ - 1);
            double v_f = (max_el_ - el) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (v_res_ - 1);

            int u = static_cast<int>(std::round(u_f));
            int v = static_cast<int>(std::round(v_f));
            u = clampValue(u, 0, h_res_-1);
            v = clampValue(v, 0, v_res_-1);

            int pix = v * h_res_ + u;
            PixAcc &p = acc[pix];

            p.sum += intensity;
            p.count += 1;
            p.sum_x += x; p.sum_y += y; p.sum_z += z;
            if (p.count == 1 || intensity > p.max_val) {
                p.max_val = intensity;
                p.max_x = x; p.max_y = y; p.max_z = z;
            }
            has_point[pix] = 1;
        }

        auto t_proj = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // build intensity image and pixel->3D representative arrays (use max fusion if chosen)
        cv::Mat intensity_f(v_res_, h_res_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
        std::vector<float> pixel_x((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0f);
        std::vector<float> pixel_y((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0f);
        std::vector<float> pixel_z((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0f);
        std::vector<char> has_pt((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0);

        bool use_max = (fusion_mode_ == "max" || fusion_mode_ == "MAX");

        for (int r = 0; r < v_res_; ++r) {
            for (int c = 0; c < h_res_; ++c) {
                int pix = r * h_res_ + c;
                PixAcc &p = acc[pix];
                if (p.count == 0) {
                    intensity_f.at<float>(r,c) = 0.0f;
                    has_pt[pix] = 0;
                } else {
                    if (use_max) {
                        intensity_f.at<float>(r,c) = p.max_val;
                        pixel_x[pix] = p.max_x; pixel_y[pix] = p.max_y; pixel_z[pix] = p.max_z;
                    } else {
                        intensity_f.at<float>(r,c) = p.sum / static_cast<float>(p.count);
                        pixel_x[pix] = p.sum_x / p.count; pixel_y[pix] = p.sum_y / p.count; pixel_z[pix] = p.sum_z / p.count;
                    }
                    has_pt[pix] = 1;
                }
            }
        }

        // normalize to 8U
        cv::Mat intensity_8u;
        cv::normalize(intensity_f, intensity_8u, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        auto t_norm = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // contrast + blur
        cv::Mat intensity_enh = intensity_8u.clone();
        if (contrast_mode_ == "equalize") {
            cv::equalizeHist(intensity_8u, intensity_enh);
        } else if (contrast_mode_ == "clahe") {
            cv::Ptr<cv::CLAHE> clahe = cv::createCLAHE(3.0, cv::Size(8,8));
            clahe->apply(intensity_8u, intensity_enh);
        }
        if (enable_blur_) cv::GaussianBlur(intensity_enh, intensity_enh, cv::Size(3,3), 0);

        auto t_pre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // save images
        std::string base = output_dir_ + "/";
        std::string raw_name = base + "intensity_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png";
        std::string col_name = base + "color_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png";
        cv::imwrite(raw_name, intensity_8u);
        cv::Mat colmap; cv::applyColorMap(intensity_enh, colmap, cv::COLORMAP_JET);
        cv::imwrite(col_name, colmap);

        auto t_saveimg = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // ORB extract
        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(
            orb_nfeatures_, static_cast<float>(orb_scaleFactor_), orb_nlevels_,
            orb_edgeThreshold_, 0, orb_wta_k_, orb_score_type_,
            orb_patchSize_, orb_fastThreshold_
        );

        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        auto t_orb_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_enh, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);
        auto t_orb_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        ROS_INFO("Frame %d: ORB keypoints=%zu, descriptors=%dx%d", frame_idx_, keypoints.size(), descriptors.rows, descriptors.cols);

        // matching with previous
        if (has_prev_frame_ && !prev_desc_.empty() && !descriptors.empty()) {
            // knn + ratio
            cv::BFMatcher matcher(cv::NORM_HAMMING);
            std::vector<std::vector<cv::DMatch>> knn;
            matcher.knnMatch(prev_desc_, descriptors, knn, 2);

            std::vector<cv::DMatch> good; good.reserve(knn.size());
            for (size_t i=0;i<knn.size();++i) {
                if (knn[i].size() < 2) continue;
                const cv::DMatch &m1 = knn[i][0];
                const cv::DMatch &m2 = knn[i][1];
                if (m1.distance < ratio_thresh_ * m2.distance) good.push_back(m1);
            }

            // distance threshold
            std::vector<cv::DMatch> filt;
            for (const auto &m : good) if (m.distance <= hamming_thresh_) filt.push_back(m);

            // 2D RANSAC (Homography) to remove geometric outliers
            std::vector<cv::Point2f> pts1, pts2;
            pts1.reserve(filt.size()); pts2.reserve(filt.size());
            for (const auto &m : filt) {
                pts1.push_back(prev_kp_[m.queryIdx].pt);
                pts2.push_back(keypoints[m.trainIdx].pt);
            }

            std::vector<uchar> inliers2d;
            if (pts1.size() >= 8) {
                cv::findHomography(pts1, pts2, cv::RANSAC, ransac_2d_reproj_, inliers2d);
            } else {
                inliers2d.assign(pts1.size(), 1);
            }

            std::vector<cv::DMatch> final_matches;
            for (size_t i=0;i<filt.size();++i) if (inliers2d[i]) final_matches.push_back(filt[i]);

            ROS_INFO("Matches: raw_knn=%zu good=%zu filt=%zu final2d=%zu", knn.size(), good.size(), filt.size(), final_matches.size());

            // Build 3D-3D correspondence from final_matches using prev_pixel_x/y/z and current pixel_x/y/z
            std::vector<cv::Point3f> P_all, Q_all; // P: prev, Q: current
            P_all.reserve(final_matches.size());
            Q_all.reserve(final_matches.size());

            for (const auto &m : final_matches) {
                // prev keypoint -> pixel
                cv::Point2f kp_prev = prev_kp_[m.queryIdx].pt;
                int u_prev = static_cast<int>(std::round(kp_prev.x));
                int v_prev = static_cast<int>(std::round(kp_prev.y));
                u_prev = clampValue(u_prev, 0, h_res_-1);
                v_prev = clampValue(v_prev, 0, v_res_-1);
                int pix_prev = v_prev * h_res_ + u_prev;

                // curr keypoint -> pixel
                cv::Point2f kp_cur = keypoints[m.trainIdx].pt;
                int u_cur = static_cast<int>(std::round(kp_cur.x));
                int v_cur = static_cast<int>(std::round(kp_cur.y));
                u_cur = clampValue(u_cur, 0, h_res_-1);
                v_cur = clampValue(v_cur, 0, v_res_-1);
                int pix_cur = v_cur * h_res_ + u_cur;

                // check has point
                if (prev_has_point_.size() == 0 || prev_has_point_[pix_prev] == 0) continue;
                if (has_pt[pix_cur] == 0) continue;

                // get 3D
                cv::Point3f P(prev_pixel_x_[pix_prev], prev_pixel_y_[pix_prev], prev_pixel_z_[pix_prev]);
                cv::Point3f Q(pixel_x[pix_cur], pixel_y[pix_cur], pixel_z[pix_cur]);
                // sanity check for NaN
                if (!std::isfinite(P.x) || !std::isfinite(P.y) || !std::isfinite(P.z)) continue;
                if (!std::isfinite(Q.x) || !std::isfinite(Q.y) || !std::isfinite(Q.z)) continue;

                P_all.push_back(P);
                Q_all.push_back(Q);
            }

            ROS_INFO("3D correspondences from 2D inliers: %zu pairs", P_all.size());

            // If enough correspondences, run 3D-RANSAC (SVD) to get rigid transform
            cv::Mat bestR, bestt;
            std::vector<char> best_inliers3d;
            if (P_all.size() >= 3) {
                ransac3D(P_all, Q_all, ransac_3d_thresh_, ransac_3d_iters_, bestR, bestt, best_inliers3d);
                // count inliers
                size_t inlier_cnt = 0;
                for (auto v : best_inliers3d) if (v) inlier_cnt++;
                ROS_INFO("3D-RANSAC inliers: %zu / %zu (thresh=%.3f m, iters=%d)",
                         inlier_cnt, P_all.size(), ransac_3d_thresh_, ransac_3d_iters_);

                if (inlier_cnt >= 3) {
                    // Save T_guess to file (4x4)
                    cv::Mat T = cv::Mat::eye(4,4,CV_64F);
                    if (!bestR.empty() && !bestt.empty()) {
                        bestR.copyTo(T(cv::Rect(0,0,3,3)));
                        T.at<double>(0,3) = bestt.at<double>(0,0);
                        T.at<double>(1,3) = bestt.at<double>(1,0);
                        T.at<double>(2,3) = bestt.at<double>(2,0);
                    }
                    // write file
                    std::string tfile = base + "T_guess_frame_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_-1) + "_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".txt";
                    std::ofstream ofs(tfile, std::ios::trunc);
                    if (ofs.is_open()) {
                        ofs << "# T_guess 4x4 (row-major) from frame " << frame_idx_-1 << " to " << frame_idx_ << "\n";
                        for (int r=0;r<4;++r) {
                            for (int c=0;c<4;++c) {
                                ofs << T.at<double>(r,c) << (c==3? "":" ");
                            }
                            ofs << "\n";
                        }
                        ofs.close();
                        ROS_INFO("Saved T_guess to %s", tfile.c_str());
                    } else {
                        ROS_WARN("Cannot open %s to save T_guess", tfile.c_str());
                    }
                } else {
                    ROS_WARN("Not enough 3D inliers to produce T_guess");
                }
            } else {
                ROS_WARN("Not enough 3D correspondences (%zu) to run 3D-RANSAC", P_all.size());
            }

            // --- produce vertical match visualization using final 2D inliers (final_matches) ---
            if ( (frame_idx_ % save_match_vis_every_) == 0 ) {
                int w = std::max(prev_img_.cols, intensity_enh.cols);
                int h = prev_img_.rows + intensity_enh.rows;
                cv::Mat vis(h, w, CV_8UC3, cv::Scalar(0,0,0));
                cv::Mat top, bottom; cv::cvtColor(prev_img_, top, cv::COLOR_GRAY2BGR);
                cv::cvtColor(intensity_enh, bottom, cv::COLOR_GRAY2BGR);
                top.copyTo(vis(cv::Rect(0,0,top.cols, top.rows)));
                bottom.copyTo(vis(cv::Rect(0, top.rows, bottom.cols, bottom.rows)));

                for (const auto &m : final_matches) {
                    cv::Point2f a = prev_kp_[m.queryIdx].pt;
                    cv::Point2f b = keypoints[m.trainIdx].pt + cv::Point2f(0, (float)top.rows);
                    cv::Scalar color(0, 255, 0);
                    cv::line(vis, a, b, color, 1, cv::LINE_AA);
                    cv::circle(vis, a, 2, cv::Scalar(0,0,255), -1);
                    cv::circle(vis, b, 2, cv::Scalar(0,255,255), -1);
                }
                std::string match_path = base + "match_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png";
                cv::imwrite(match_path, vis);
                ROS_INFO("Saved match visualization: %s", match_path.c_str());
            }
        } // end if has_prev_frame

        // store current as previous for next frame
        prev_img_ = intensity_8u.clone();
        prev_img_f_ = intensity_f.clone();
        prev_desc_ = descriptors.clone();
        prev_kp_ = keypoints;
        prev_pixel_x_ = pixel_x;
        prev_pixel_y_ = pixel_y;
        prev_pixel_z_ = pixel_z;
        prev_has_point_ = has_pt;
        has_prev_frame_ = true;

        auto t_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        double total_ms = std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(t_end - t0).count();

        ROS_INFO("Frame %d processed. Total time %.1f ms", frame_idx_, total_ms);
        frame_idx_++;
    }

}; // end class

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_intensity_orb_match_tguess");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");
    LidarIntensityORBMatchT node(nh);
    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}






// lidar_intensity_dvo_node.cpp
// LiDAR Intensity Direct Visual Odometry (photometric + geometric joint optimization)
// 说明：
//  - 将 pointcloud 投影成 intensity 图 (按角度映射)，并记录每像素代表 3D 点（最大强度点）。
//  - 对连续两帧：把 prev 的每个像素反投影 -> 变换（pose） -> 投影到 cur，
//    若在 cur 有代表 3D 点则构建残差：photometric + geometric (3D)。
//  - 使用 Ceres (AutoDiff) 优化 6DoF pose（angle-axis + translation）。
//
// 编译依赖：ceres, OpenCV, ROS
// 注意：运行前在 launch/param 中设置 output_dir, v_res, h_res 等参数（代码中有默认值）.

#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <ceres/ceres.h>
#include <ceres/rotation.h>

#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>
#include <chrono>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>

template<typename T>
inline T clampValueT(T v, T lo, T hi) { return (v < lo) ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

inline int clampInt(int v, int lo, int hi) { return (v < lo) ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

// ----------------------------
// Residual functor (AutoDiff)
// residuals: [ w_photo*(Icur_sample - Iprev), w_geo*(X - Xs), w_geo*(Y - Ys), w_geo*(Z - Zs) ]
// Note: we sample Icur and (Xs,Ys,Zs) via bilinear interpolation from arrays passed in as doubles.
// ----------------------------
struct PhotometricGeometricFunctor {
    // prev point and prev intensity (per-residual constants)
    double prev_x_, prev_y_, prev_z_;
    double I_prev_;

    // pointers to current-frame images/3D arrays (stored as row-major arrays of size H*W)
    const double* cur_intensity_; // length = H*W (normalized 0..255)
    const double* cur_px_;
    const double* cur_py_;
    const double* cur_pz_;
    int W_, H_;
    // angular projection params (for LiDAR)
    double min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_;

    double w_photo_, w_geo_; // weights

    PhotometricGeometricFunctor(double px, double py, double pz, double Iprev,
                                const double* cur_int, const double* curpx, const double* curpy, const double* curpz,
                                int W, int H,
                                double min_az, double max_az, double min_el, double max_el,
                                double w_photo, double w_geo)
        : prev_x_(px), prev_y_(py), prev_z_(pz), I_prev_(Iprev),
          cur_intensity_(cur_int), cur_px_(curpx), cur_py_(curpy), cur_pz_(curpz),
          W_(W), H_(H),
          min_az_(min_az), max_az_(max_az), min_el_(min_el), max_el_(max_el),
          w_photo_(w_photo), w_geo_(w_geo) {}

    template <typename T>
    bool operator()(const T* const pose /*angle-axis (3), trans(3)*/, T* residual) const {
        // 1) transform prev point by pose
        T p[3];
        p[0] = T(prev_x_); p[1] = T(prev_y_); p[2] = T(prev_z_);
        T p_trans[3];
        // angle-axis rotate
        ceres::AngleAxisRotatePoint(pose, p, p_trans);
        // translate
        p_trans[0] += pose[3];
        p_trans[1] += pose[4];
        p_trans[2] += pose[5];

        // 2) project to spherical/angular image to get (u', v') coords
        // compute azimuth & elevation
        T X = p_trans[0], Y = p_trans[1], Z = p_trans[2];
        // avoid division by zero
        T rho = ceres::sqrt(X*X + Y*Y);
        // az = atan2(y,x), el = atan2(z, sqrt(x^2+y^2))
        T az = ceres::atan2(Y, X);
        T el = ceres::atan2(Z, rho);

        // normalize to [0, W-1], [0, H-1] using stored double constants
        T u_f = (az - T(min_az_)) / T(max_az_ - min_az_) * T(W_ - 1);
        T v_f = (T(max_el_) - el) / T(max_el_ - min_el_) * T(H_ - 1);

        // bilinear interpolation: compute floor indices and weights
        T u0 = ceres::floor(u_f);
        T v0 = ceres::floor(v_f);
        T du = u_f - u0;
        T dv = v_f - v0;

        int u0i = (int)std::floor(u0); // these casts are safe because we check feasibility before adding cost
        int v0i = (int)std::floor(v0);

        // bounds check: if outside, set residuals to zero (shouldn't happen if pre-checked)
        if (u0i < 0 || v0i < 0 || u0i+1 >= W_ || v0i+1 >= H_) {
            // Set residuals to zero (this path should be avoided; we pre-filter)
            residual[0] = T(0);
            residual[1] = T(0);
            residual[2] = T(0);
            residual[3] = T(0);
            return true;
        }

        // indices for 4 neighbors
        int idx00 = v0i * W_ + u0i;
        int idx01 = v0i * W_ + (u0i+1);
        int idx10 = (v0i+1) * W_ + u0i;
        int idx11 = (v0i+1) * W_ + (u0i+1);

        // sample Icur via bilinear: value = (1-du)*(1-dv)*I00 + du*(1-dv)*I01 + (1-du)*dv*I10 + du*dv*I11
        T I00 = T(cur_intensity_[idx00]);
        T I01 = T(cur_intensity_[idx01]);
        T I10 = T(cur_intensity_[idx10]);
        T I11 = T(cur_intensity_[idx11]);
        T Icur = (T(1)-du)*(T(1)-dv)*I00 + du*(T(1)-dv)*I01 + (T(1)-du)*dv*I10 + du*dv*I11;

        // sample current 3D (px,py,pz) via bilinear interpolation similarly
        T X00 = T(cur_px_[idx00]), X01 = T(cur_px_[idx01]), X10 = T(cur_px_[idx10]), X11 = T(cur_px_[idx11]);
        T Y00 = T(cur_py_[idx00]), Y01 = T(cur_py_[idx01]), Y10 = T(cur_py_[idx10]), Y11 = T(cur_py_[idx11]);
        T Z00 = T(cur_pz_[idx00]), Z01 = T(cur_pz_[idx01]), Z10 = T(cur_pz_[idx10]), Z11 = T(cur_pz_[idx11]);

        T Xs = (T(1)-du)*(T(1)-dv)*X00 + du*(T(1)-dv)*X01 + (T(1)-du)*dv*X10 + du*dv*X11;
        T Ys = (T(1)-du)*(T(1)-dv)*Y00 + du*(T(1)-dv)*Y01 + (T(1)-du)*dv*Y10 + du*dv*Y11;
        T Zs = (T(1)-du)*(T(1)-dv)*Z00 + du*(T(1)-dv)*Z01 + (T(1)-du)*dv*Z10 + du*dv*Z11;

        // photometric residual (scaled)
        residual[0] = T(w_photo_) * (Icur - T(I_prev_));

        // geometric residual: vector difference
        residual[1] = T(w_geo_) * (X - Xs);
        residual[2] = T(w_geo_) * (Y - Ys);
        residual[3] = T(w_geo_) * (Z - Zs);

        return true;
    }
};

// ----------------------------
// 主节点类
// ----------------------------
class LidarIntensityDVO {
public:
    LidarIntensityDVO(ros::NodeHandle& nh)
        : nh_(nh), frame_idx_(0), has_prev_(false)
    {
        // --- 参数（可由 rosparam 覆盖） ---
        nh_.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, std::string("/tmp/lidar_dvo_output"));
        nh_.param<int>("v_res", v_res_, 128);
        nh_.param<int>("h_res", h_res_, 500);
        nh_.param<double>("h_fov_deg", h_fov_deg_, 120.0);
        nh_.param<double>("v_min_deg", v_min_deg_, -12.5);
        nh_.param<double>("v_max_deg", v_max_deg_, 12.9);
        nh_.param<int>("sample_step", sample_step_, 5); // 在 prev 图像上每 sample_step 个像素添加一个残差（降低计算量）
        nh_.param<double>("w_photo", w_photo_, 1.0);
        nh_.param<double>("w_geo", w_geo_, 1.0);
        nh_.param<int>("min_matches", min_matches_, 30); // 最低残差数量

        // 创建输出目录
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST) {
            ROS_WARN("mkdir failed or exists: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }

        // 角度边界（弧度）
        min_az_ = -h_fov_deg_/2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_az_ =  h_fov_deg_/2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
