        min_el_ = v_min_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_el_ = v_max_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;

        sub_ = nh_.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarIntensityDVO::cloudCallback, this);

        ROS_INFO("LiDAR Intensity DVO node started. output_dir=%s", output_dir_.c_str());
    }

private:
    ros::NodeHandle nh_;
    ros::Subscriber sub_;

    // params
    std::string output_dir_;
    int v_res_, h_res_;
    double h_fov_deg_, v_min_deg_, v_max_deg_;
    double min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_;
    int sample_step_;
    double w_photo_, w_geo_;
    int min_matches_;

    // previous frame buffers
    bool has_prev_;
    cv::Mat prev_intensity_8u_; // enhanced or raw 8U
    std::vector<double> prev_px_, prev_py_, prev_pz_;
    std::vector<char> prev_has_;

    // current frame temp buffers
    std::vector<double> pixel_x, pixel_y, pixel_z;
    std::vector<char> has_point;
    cv::Mat cur_intensity_8u_;

    int frame_idx_;

    // callback: consumes one PointCloud2 frame, does projection, and runs DVO between prev & cur
    void cloudCallback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg) {
        if (!msg) return;
        auto t_all_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // 1) 投影：把点云转换为 intensity image（float）, 并记录每像素的代表 3D 点（最大强度）
        std::vector<double> grid_px((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<double> grid_py((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<double> grid_pz((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<double> grid_int((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<char> grid_has((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0);

        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_x(*msg,"x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_y(*msg,"y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_z(*msg,"z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_i(*msg,"intensity");

        int idx = 0;
        for (; it_x != it_x.end(); ++it_x, ++it_y, ++it_z, ++it_i, ++idx) {
            float x = *it_x, y = *it_y, z = *it_z, inten = *it_i;
            if (!std::isfinite(x) || !std::isfinite(y) || !std::isfinite(z) || !std::isfinite(inten)) continue;
            double az = std::atan2((double)y, (double)x);
            double el = std::atan2((double)z, std::sqrt((double)x*(double)x + (double)y*(double)y));
            if (az < min_az_ || az > max_az_ || el < min_el_ || el > max_el_) continue;
            int u = (int)std::round((az - min_az_) / (max_az_ - min_az_) * (h_res_ - 1));
            int v = (int)std::round((max_el_ - el) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (v_res_ - 1));
            u = clampInt(u, 0, h_res_-1); v = clampInt(v, 0, v_res_-1);
            int id = v * h_res_ + u;
            // keep max intensity representative
            if (!grid_has[id] || inten > grid_int[id]) {
                grid_int[id] = inten;
                grid_px[id] = x; grid_py[id] = y; grid_pz[id] = z;
                grid_has[id] = 1;
            }
        }

        // build float image and per-pixel 3d arrays
        cv::Mat intensity_f(v_res_, h_res_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
        pixel_x.assign((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        pixel_y.assign((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        pixel_z.assign((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        has_point.assign((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_, 0);

        for (int r = 0; r < v_res_; ++r) {
            for (int c = 0; c < h_res_; ++c) {
                int id = r * h_res_ + c;
                if (grid_has[id]) {
                    intensity_f.at<float>(r,c) = (float)grid_int[id];
                    pixel_x[id] = grid_px[id];
                    pixel_y[id] = grid_py[id];
                    pixel_z[id] = grid_pz[id];
                    has_point[id] = 1;
                } else {
                    intensity_f.at<float>(r,c) = 0.0f;
                }
            }
        }

        // convert intensity to 8U (normalized) for visualization and photometric stability
        cv::Mat intensity_8u;
        cv::normalize(intensity_f, intensity_8u, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);

        // optional: apply CLAHE to enhance contrast (help photometric matching)
        cv::Mat intensity_enh = intensity_8u.clone();
        cv::Ptr<cv::CLAHE> clahe = cv::createCLAHE(3.0, cv::Size(8,8));
        clahe->apply(intensity_8u, intensity_enh);

        // save current frame buffers
        cur_intensity_8u_ = intensity_enh.clone();

        // 2) 如果没有上一帧，缓存当前并返回
        if (!has_prev_) {
            // cache prev data
            prev_intensity_8u_ = cur_intensity_8u_.clone();
            prev_px_ = pixel_x; prev_py_ = pixel_y; prev_pz_ = pixel_z; prev_has_ = has_point;
            has_prev_ = true;
            frame_idx_++;
            ROS_INFO("Init prev frame stored.");
            return;
        }

        // 3) 构建 Ceres 问题：对 prev 图像中每 sample_step 的像素建立 residuals
        // pose param: angle-axis (3) + translation (3). 初值设为零（或可由 ORB-Tguess 提供）
        double pose[6] = {0,0,0, 0,0,0}; // angle-axis + translation
        ceres::Problem problem;

        // To avoid adding residuals which will be invalid after auto-diff (outside img / no cur 3D),
        // we use initial pose to precompute where each prev point would land, and only add residuals
        // if that location is inside and cur has 3D data.
        int added = 0;
        // precompute pointers for Ceres functors
        // convert cur_intensity_8u_ to double array row-major
        std::vector<double> cur_int_double((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_);
        std::vector<double> cur_px_double((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_);
        std::vector<double> cur_py_double((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_);
        std::vector<double> cur_pz_double((size_t)v_res_ * h_res_);

        for (int r=0;r<v_res_;++r) for (int c=0;c<h_res_;++c) {
            int id=r*h_res_+c;
            cur_int_double[id] = (double)intensity_enh.at<unsigned char>(r,c);
            cur_px_double[id] = pixel_x[id];
            cur_py_double[id] = pixel_y[id];
            cur_pz_double[id] = pixel_z[id];
        }

        // for speed, reference to arrays
        const double* cur_int_ptr = cur_int_double.data();
        const double* cur_px_ptr = cur_px_double.data();
        const double* cur_py_ptr = cur_py_double.data();
        const double* cur_pz_ptr = cur_pz_double.data();

        // iterate prev image pixels with sampling
        for (int r = 0; r < v_res_; r += 1) {
            for (int c = 0; c < h_res_; c += sample_step_) {
                int id = r * h_res_ + c;
                if (!prev_has_[id]) continue; // no valid prev 3d
                double px = prev_px_[id], py = prev_py_[id], pz = prev_pz_[id];
                double Iprev = (double)prev_intensity_8u_.at<unsigned char>(r,c);

                // use initial pose (identity) to project -> decide if cur has correspondence
                // transform prev point with initial pose (identity) -> so p_trans = prev point
                double X = px, Y = py, Z = pz;
                // project
                double az = atan2(Y, X);
                double el = atan2(Z, sqrt(X*X + Y*Y));
                if (az < min_az_ || az > max_az_ || el < min_el_ || el > max_el_) continue;
                double uf = (az - min_az_) / (max_az_ - min_az_) * (h_res_ - 1);
                double vf = (max_el_ - el) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (v_res_ - 1);
                int ui = (int)std::round(uf), vi = (int)std::round(vf);
                if (ui < 0 || ui >= h_res_ || vi < 0 || vi >= v_res_) continue;
                int id2 = vi * h_res_ + ui;
                if (!has_point[id2]) continue; // no cur 3d representative -> skip

                // Add a residual block (photometric + geometric vector of size 4)
                PhotometricGeometricFunctor* functor = new PhotometricGeometricFunctor(
                    px, py, pz, Iprev,
                    cur_int_ptr, cur_px_ptr, cur_py_ptr, cur_pz_ptr,
                    h_res_, v_res_,
                    min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_,
                    w_photo_, w_geo_);

                ceres::CostFunction* cost = new ceres::AutoDiffCostFunction<PhotometricGeometricFunctor, 4, 6>(functor);

                // robust loss
                ceres::LossFunction* loss = new ceres::HuberLoss(1.0);

                problem.AddResidualBlock(cost, loss, pose);
                added++;
            }
        }

        ROS_INFO("Frame %d: added %d residual blocks (sample_step=%d)", frame_idx_, added, sample_step_);

        if (added < min_matches_) {
            ROS_WARN("Too few residuals (%d), skip optimization this frame.", added);
            // update prev buffer and return
            prev_intensity_8u_ = cur_intensity_8u_.clone();
            prev_px_ = pixel_x; prev_py_ = pixel_y; prev_pz_ = pixel_z; prev_has_ = has_point;
            frame_idx_++;
            return;
        }

        // set solver options and solve
        ceres::Solver::Options options;
        options.max_num_iterations = 50;
        options.minimizer_progress_to_stdout = false;
        options.linear_solver_type = ceres::DENSE_QR;

        ceres::Solver::Summary summary;
        auto t_opt_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        ceres::Solve(options, &problem, &summary);
        auto t_opt_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        double opt_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_opt_end - t_opt_start).count();

        ROS_INFO("Ceres done. Iterations: %d, InitialCost: %.6f, FinalCost: %.6f, time=%.1fms",
                 (int)summary.iterations.size(),
                 summary.initial_cost, summary.final_cost, opt_ms);

        // output optimized pose (angle-axis + translation)
        double rx = pose[0], ry = pose[1], rz = pose[2];
        double tx = pose[3], ty = pose[4], tz = pose[5];
        // convert angle-axis to roll/pitch/yaw for logging (approx)
        double Rmat[9];
        double aa[3] = {rx, ry, rz};
        double pt[3] = {1.0, 0.0, 0.0};
        ceres::AngleAxisToRotationMatrix(aa, Rmat); // Rmat is column-major 3x3? ceres provides column-major 3x3
        // Use Rmat to compute yaw/pitch/roll (ZYX)
        // Construct as Eigen would; but approximate using formulas:
        double r11 = Rmat[0], r12 = Rmat[3], r13 = Rmat[6];
        double r21 = Rmat[1], r22 = Rmat[4], r23 = Rmat[7];
        double r31 = Rmat[2], r32 = Rmat[5], r33 = Rmat[8];

        double yaw = atan2(r21, r11);
        double pitch = atan2(-r31, sqrt(r32*r32 + r33*r33));
        double roll = atan2(r32, r33);

        ROS_INFO("Optimized pose (angle-axis): [%.6f %.6f %.6f], trans=[%.6f %.6f %.6f], rpy(deg)=[%.3f %.3f %.3f]",
                 rx, ry, rz, tx, ty, tz, roll*180.0/M_PI, pitch*180.0/M_PI, yaw*180.0/M_PI);

        // append to log file
        {
            std::ofstream ofs(output_dir_ + "/dvo_T_log.txt", std::ios::app);
            ofs << frame_idx_ << " " << tx << " " << ty << " " << tz << " "
                << roll*180.0/M_PI << " " << pitch*180.0/M_PI << " " << yaw*180.0/M_PI << " "
                << "res_blocks=" << added << " opt_ms=" << opt_ms << "\n";
            ofs.close();
        }

        // 4) 可视化：保存 prev warped by optimized pose to compare (optional)
        // We'll warp prev intensity into cur frame using optimized pose and save side-by-side images
        cv::Mat prev_warped(v_res_, h_res_, CV_8U, cv::Scalar(0));
        // For each prev pixel -> transform by optimized pose -> project -> put intensity
        for (int r=0;r<v_res_;++r){
            for (int c=0;c<h_res_;++c){
                int id = r*h_res_ + c;
                if (!prev_has_[id]) continue;
                double px = prev_px_[id], py = prev_py_[id], pz = prev_pz_[id];
                double p_in[3] = {px, py, pz};
                double p_out[3];
                ceres::AngleAxisRotatePoint(pose, p_in, p_out);
                p_out[0] += pose[3]; p_out[1] += pose[4]; p_out[2] += pose[5];
                double az = atan2(p_out[1], p_out[0]);
                double el = atan2(p_out[2], sqrt(p_out[0]*p_out[0] + p_out[1]*p_out[1]));
                if (az < min_az_ || az > max_az_ || el < min_el_ || el > max_el_) continue;
                double uf = (az - min_az_) / (max_az_ - min_az_) * (h_res_ - 1);
                double vf = (max_el_ - el) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (v_res_ - 1);
                int ui = (int)std::round(uf), vi = (int)std::round(vf);
                if (ui<0||ui>=h_res_||vi<0||vi>=v_res_) continue;
                prev_warped.at<unsigned char>(vi, ui) = (unsigned char)prev_intensity_8u_.at<unsigned char>(r,c);
            }
        }
        cv::Mat vis;
        cv::hconcat(prev_warped, cur_intensity_8u_, vis);
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/warp_prev_cur_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png", vis);

        // 5) update prev buffers
        prev_intensity_8u_ = cur_intensity_8u_.clone();
        prev_px_ = pixel_x; prev_py_ = pixel_y; prev_pz_ = pixel_z; prev_has_ = has_point;
        has_prev_ = true;
        frame_idx_++;

        auto t_all_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        double all_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_all_end - t_all_start).count();
        ROS_INFO("Frame processed total time: %.1f ms", all_ms);
    }
};

// ----------------------------
// main
// ----------------------------
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_intensity_dvo");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");
    LidarIntensityDVO node(nh);
    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}



#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>
#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <ceres/ceres.h>
#include <ceres/rotation.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <fstream>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>

// =========================== 辅助结构体 ===========================

// 光度 + 几何残差
struct PhotometricGeometricResidual {
    PhotometricGeometricResidual(cv::Point3f p_prev, float intensity_prev,
                                 cv::Point3f p_cur, float intensity_cur, double w_photo, double w_geo)
        : p_prev_(p_prev), I_prev_(intensity_prev), p_cur_(p_cur), I_cur_(intensity_cur),
          w_photo_(w_photo), w_geo_(w_geo) {}

    template <typename T>
    bool operator()(const T* const pose, T* residual) const {
        // pose: [rx, ry, rz, tx, ty, tz]
        T p[3] = {T(p_prev_.x), T(p_prev_.y), T(p_prev_.z)};
        T p_trans[3];
        ceres::AngleAxisRotatePoint(pose, p, p_trans);
        p_trans[0] += pose[3];
        p_trans[1] += pose[4];
        p_trans[2] += pose[5];

        // 计算几何误差（欧式距离）
        T dx = p_trans[0] - T(p_cur_.x);
        T dy = p_trans[1] - T(p_cur_.y);
        T dz = p_trans[2] - T(p_cur_.z);
        T geo_err = ceres::sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);

        // 光度误差
        T photo_err = T(I_cur_ - I_prev_);

        residual[0] = T(w_photo_) * photo_err + T(w_geo_) * geo_err;
        return true;
    }

    cv::Point3f p_prev_, p_cur_;
    float I_prev_, I_cur_;
    double w_photo_, w_geo_;
};

// =========================== 主类 ===========================
class LidarIntensityDVO {
public:
    LidarIntensityDVO(ros::NodeHandle& nh)
        : nh_(nh), has_prev_(false)
    {
        nh_.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "./lidar_dvo_output");
        nh_.param<double>("w_photo", w_photo_, 1.0);
        nh_.param<double>("w_geo", w_geo_, 0.5);
        nh_.param<double>("max_dist", max_dist_, 30.0);
        nh_.param<int>("sample_step", sample_step_, 3);

        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST)
            ROS_WARN("mkdir failed: %s", output_dir_.c_str());

        sub_ = nh_.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarIntensityDVO::callback, this);

        ROS_INFO("LiDAR Intensity DVO node initialized. Output: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
    }

private:
    ros::NodeHandle nh_;
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    std::string output_dir_;
    bool has_prev_;
    double w_photo_, w_geo_;
    double max_dist_;
    int sample_step_;

    std::vector<cv::Point3f> prev_pts_;
    std::vector<float> prev_intensity_;

    // =========================== 回调函数 ===========================
    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr& msg)
    {
        std::vector<cv::Point3f> cur_pts;
        std::vector<float> cur_intensity;

        // 从消息提取 xyz + intensity
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> iter_i(*msg, "intensity");

        int idx = 0;
        for (; iter_x != iter_x.end(); ++iter_x, ++iter_y, ++iter_z, ++iter_i, ++idx) {
            if (sample_step_ > 1 && idx % sample_step_ != 0) continue;
            float x = *iter_x, y = *iter_y, z = *iter_z, I = *iter_i;
            float dist = std::sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);
            if (dist > max_dist_) continue;
            cur_pts.emplace_back(x, y, z);
            cur_intensity.push_back(I);
        }

        ROS_INFO("Received frame with %zu valid points", cur_pts.size());
        if (!has_prev_) {
            prev_pts_ = cur_pts;
            prev_intensity_ = cur_intensity;
            has_prev_ = true;
            return;
        }

        auto t0 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // 设置 Ceres 优化问题
        double pose[6] = {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}; // 初始估计
        ceres::Problem problem;
        ceres::LossFunction* loss = new ceres::HuberLoss(1.0);

        for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(prev_pts_.size(), cur_pts.size()); ++i) {
            ceres::CostFunction* cost = new ceres::AutoDiffCostFunction<
                PhotometricGeometricResidual, 1, 6>(
                new PhotometricGeometricResidual(prev_pts_[i], prev_intensity_[i],
                                                 cur_pts[i], cur_intensity[i],
                                                 w_photo_, w_geo_));
            problem.AddResidualBlock(cost, loss, pose);
        }

        // 求解
        ceres::Solver::Options options;
        options.linear_solver_type = ceres::DENSE_QR;
        options.minimizer_progress_to_stdout = false;
        options.max_num_iterations = 50;
        ceres::Solver::Summary summary;
        ceres::Solve(options, &problem, &summary);

        auto t1 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        double dt = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t1 - t0).count();

        // 输出结果
        double rx=pose[0], ry=pose[1], rz=pose[2], tx=pose[3], ty=pose[4], tz=pose[5];
        double roll=rx*180/M_PI, pitch=ry*180/M_PI, yaw=rz*180/M_PI;
        ROS_INFO("Optimized pose: t=(%.3f, %.3f, %.3f) RPY=(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f) dt=%.1fms",
                 tx, ty, tz, roll, pitch, yaw, dt);

        std::ofstream ofs(output_dir_ + "/T_dvo_log.txt", std::ios::app);
        ofs << tx << " " << ty << " " << tz << " " << roll << " " << pitch << " " << yaw << "\n";
        ofs.close();

        // 更新缓存
        prev_pts_ = cur_pts;
        prev_intensity_ = cur_intensity;
    }
};

// =========================== 主函数 ===========================
int main(int argc, char** argv)
{
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_intensity_dvo");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");
    LidarIntensityDVO node(nh);
    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}

/******************************************************
 *  LiDAR Intensity Direct Visual Odometry (DVO) - MT
 *  多线程优化版
 *  变更点：
 *    1) 并行化残差块构建（OpenMP）
 *    2) Ceres 多线程求解（num_threads / num_linear_solver_threads）
 *    3) 并行化 double 数组准备（强度与坐标表）
 *    4) 增加 num_threads 参数（<=0 自动检测硬件线程数）
 *
 *  其余逻辑与原始实现一致：
 *     - 点云按方位角/俯仰角映射到强度图与3D表
 *     - 构建光度+几何联合残差
 *     - 使用 Ceres 优化 6DoF 位姿（角轴+平移）
 ******************************************************/

#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <ceres/ceres.h>
#include <ceres/rotation.h>

#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>

#include <fstream>
#include <chrono>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>
#include <thread> // std::thread::hardware_concurrency

// =============== 辅助函数 ===============
inline int clampInt(int v, int lo, int hi)
{
    return (v < lo) ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v);
}

template <typename T>
inline double getDoubleValue(const T &x) { return static_cast<double>(x); }

template <int N>
inline double getDoubleValue(const ceres::Jet<double, N> &x) { return x.a; }

// =============== 光度 + 几何残差 ===============
// 与原始版本一致（仅补充注释），保留 AutoDiff + 双线性插值形式
struct PhotometricGeometricResidual
{
    // 上一帧 3D & 强度
    double px_, py_, pz_;
    double I_prev_;

    // 当前帧插值所需的数据（行主序）
    const double *cur_intensity_; // double 版强度图（由 8U 转 double）
    const double *cur_x_;
    const double *cur_y_;
    const double *cur_z_;
    int W_, H_;

    // LiDAR 投影角度边界（弧度）
    double min_az_, max_az_;
    double min_el_, max_el_;

    // 残差权重（光度/几何）
    double w_photo_, w_geo_;

    PhotometricGeometricResidual(double px, double py, double pz, double Iprev,
                                 const double *cur_int, const double *curx,
                                 const double *cury, const double *curz,
                                 int W, int H,
                                 double min_az, double max_az,
                                 double min_el, double max_el,
                                 double w_photo, double w_geo)
        : px_(px), py_(py), pz_(pz), I_prev_(Iprev),
          cur_intensity_(cur_int), cur_x_(curx), cur_y_(cury), cur_z_(curz),
          W_(W), H_(H),
          min_az_(min_az), max_az_(max_az),
          min_el_(min_el), max_el_(max_el),
          w_photo_(w_photo), w_geo_(w_geo) {}

    // 残差：光度(1) + 几何(3) = 4 维
    template <typename T>
    bool operator()(const T *const pose, T *residual) const
    {
        // 1) 将上一帧3D点用当前位姿变换
        T p[3] = {T(px_), T(py_), T(pz_)};
        T p_t[3];
        ceres::AngleAxisRotatePoint(pose, p, p_t);
        p_t[0] += pose[3]; // tx
        p_t[1] += pose[4]; // ty
        p_t[2] += pose[5]; // tz

        // 2) 球面投影（与构图一致）
        T X = p_t[0], Y = p_t[1], Z = p_t[2];
        if (ceres::abs(X) < T(1e-8) && ceres::abs(Y) < T(1e-8))
        {
            residual[0] = residual[1] = residual[2] = residual[3] = T(0);
            return true;
        }
        T rho = ceres::sqrt(X * X + Y * Y);
        T az = ceres::atan2(Y, X);
        T el = ceres::atan2(Z, rho);

        // 像素坐标（浮点）
        T u_f = (az - T(min_az_)) / T(max_az_ - min_az_) * T(W_ - 1);
        T v_f = (T(max_el_) - el) / T(max_el_ - min_el_) * T(H_ - 1);

        // 3) 越界与非数检查（注意 getDoubleValue 取 Jet 标量）
        double u_fd = getDoubleValue(u_f);
        double v_fd = getDoubleValue(v_f);
        if (!std::isfinite(u_fd) || !std::isfinite(v_fd))
        {
            residual[0] = residual[1] = residual[2] = residual[3] = T(0);
            return true;
        }
        int u0 = static_cast<int>(std::floor(u_fd));
        int v0 = static_cast<int>(std::floor(v_fd));
        if (u0 < 0 || v0 < 0 || u0 + 1 >= W_ || v0 + 1 >= H_)
        {
            residual[0] = residual[1] = residual[2] = residual[3] = T(0);
            return true;
        }

        // 4) 双线性插值权重
        int idx00 = v0 * W_ + u0;
        int idx01 = v0 * W_ + (u0 + 1);
        int idx10 = (v0 + 1) * W_ + u0;
        int idx11 = (v0 + 1) * W_ + (u0 + 1);
        T du = u_f - T(u0);
        T dv = v_f - T(v0);
        T w00 = (T(1) - du) * (T(1) - dv);
        T w01 = du * (T(1) - dv);
        T w10 = (T(1) - du) * dv;
        T w11 = du * dv;

        // 5) 安全取值（当前帧缺失则当作 0；鲁棒核会抑制异常）
        auto safeFetch = [&](const double *arr, int idx) -> T
        {
            double v = arr[idx];
            return (std::isfinite(v) ? T(v) : T(0));
        };
        T Icur = w00 * safeFetch(cur_intensity_, idx00) + w01 * safeFetch(cur_intensity_, idx01) +
                 w10 * safeFetch(cur_intensity_, idx10) + w11 * safeFetch(cur_intensity_, idx11);
        T Xs = w00 * safeFetch(cur_x_, idx00) + w01 * safeFetch(cur_x_, idx01) +
               w10 * safeFetch(cur_x_, idx10) + w11 * safeFetch(cur_x_, idx11);
        T Ys = w00 * safeFetch(cur_y_, idx00) + w01 * safeFetch(cur_y_, idx01) +
               w10 * safeFetch(cur_y_, idx10) + w11 * safeFetch(cur_y_, idx11);
        T Zs = w00 * safeFetch(cur_z_, idx00) + w01 * safeFetch(cur_z_, idx01) +
               w10 * safeFetch(cur_z_, idx10) + w11 * safeFetch(cur_z_, idx11);

        // 6) 构造残差（光度 + 几何）
        residual[0] = T(w_photo_) * (Icur - T(I_prev_));
        residual[1] = T(w_geo_) * (X - Xs);
        residual[2] = T(w_geo_) * (Y - Ys);
        residual[3] = T(w_geo_) * (Z - Zs);

        // 7) 终检
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
            if (!ceres::IsFinite(residual[i]))
                residual[i] = T(0);
        return true;
    }
};

// =============== LiDAR-DVO 主类 ===============
class LidarIntensityDVO
{
public:
    LidarIntensityDVO(ros::NodeHandle &nh)
        : nh_(nh), frame_idx_(0), has_prev_(false),
          num_threads_(std::max(1u, std::thread::hardware_concurrency()))
    {

        // ---- 参数读取 ----
        nh_.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, "/home/lb/Piont_cloudToImage/lidar_dvo");
        nh_.param<int>("v_res", v_res_, 128);
        nh_.param<int>("h_res", h_res_, 500);
        nh_.param<double>("h_fov_deg", h_fov_deg_, 120.0);
        nh_.param<double>("v_min_deg", v_min_deg_, -12.5);
        nh_.param<double>("v_max_deg", v_max_deg_, 12.9);
        nh_.param<int>("sample_step", sample_step_, 1);
        nh_.param<double>("w_photo", w_photo_, 1.0);
        nh_.param<double>("w_geo", w_geo_, 1.0);
        nh_.param<int>("min_matches", min_matches_, 50);

        // 新增：线程数（<=0 则自动检测）
        int user_threads = 0;
        nh_.param<int>("num_threads", user_threads, 28);
        if (user_threads > 0)
            num_threads_ = user_threads;

        // ---- 创建输出目录 ----
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST)
            ROS_WARN("创建目录失败或已存在: %s", output_dir_.c_str());

        // ---- 投影角度边界（弧度）----
        min_az_ = -h_fov_deg_ / 2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_az_ = h_fov_deg_ / 2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        min_el_ = v_min_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_el_ = v_max_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;

        // 复用 CLAHE，避免每帧重复创建
        clahe_ = cv::createCLAHE(3.0);

        // OpenCV 线程数（不强制，交给 OpenCV 调度）
        cv::setNumThreads(num_threads_);

        sub_ = nh_.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarIntensityDVO::callback, this);
        ROS_INFO("LiDAR Intensity DVO(多线程版) 初始化完成，输出目录: %s，num_threads=%d",
                 output_dir_.c_str(), num_threads_);
    }

private:
    ros::NodeHandle nh_;
    ros::Subscriber sub_;

    // ---- 参数 ----
    std::string output_dir_;
    int v_res_, h_res_;
    double h_fov_deg_, v_min_deg_, v_max_deg_;
    double min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_;
    int sample_step_;
    double w_photo_, w_geo_;
    int min_matches_;
    int num_threads_; // 新增：线程数

    // ---- 缓存帧 ----
    bool has_prev_;
    cv::Mat prev_intensity_; // 8U CLAHE 后强度图
    std::vector<double> prev_x_, prev_y_, prev_z_;
    std::vector<char> prev_mask_;

    int frame_idx_;
    cv::Ptr<cv::CLAHE> clahe_; // 复用

    // ========== 主回调 ==========
    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg)
    {
        auto t_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // ---- 1. 投影：点云 → 强度图 + 对应3D表（与原实现一致） ----
        std::vector<double> px(v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<double> py(v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<double> pz(v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<double> intensity(v_res_ * h_res_, 0.0);
        std::vector<char> mask(v_res_ * h_res_, 0);

        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_i(*msg, "intensity");

        // 说明：ROS 的 PointCloud2 迭代器是串行游标，保持单线程遍历以确保安全
        for (; it_x != it_x.end(); ++it_x, ++it_y, ++it_z, ++it_i)
        {
            double x = *it_x, y = *it_y, z = *it_z, I = *it_i;
            if (!std::isfinite(x) || !std::isfinite(y) || !std::isfinite(z))
                continue;
            double az = atan2(y, x);
            double el = atan2(z, std::sqrt(x * x + y * y));
            if (az < min_az_ || az > max_az_ || el < min_el_ || el > max_el_)
                continue;
            int u = (int)std::round((az - min_az_) / (max_az_ - min_az_) * (h_res_ - 1));
            int v = (int)std::round((max_el_ - el) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (v_res_ - 1));
            int id = v * h_res_ + u;

            // 若该像素已有点，保留强度更大的那个（与你的原逻辑一致）
            if (!mask[id] || I > intensity[id])
            {
                px[id] = x;
                py[id] = y;
                pz[id] = z;
                intensity[id] = I;
                mask[id] = 1;
            }
        }

        // 强度图：归一化 + CLAHE（保持与原实现一致）
        cv::Mat intensity_img(v_res_, h_res_, CV_32F, intensity.data());
        cv::Mat intensity_8u;
        cv::normalize(intensity_img, intensity_8u, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        clahe_->apply(intensity_8u, intensity_8u);

        // ---- 首帧：仅缓存并返回 ----
        if (!has_prev_)
        {
            prev_intensity_ = intensity_8u.clone();
            prev_x_ = px;
            prev_y_ = py;
            prev_z_ = pz;
            prev_mask_ = mask;
            has_prev_ = true;
            frame_idx_++;
            ROS_INFO("首帧缓存完成");
            return;
        }

        // ---- 2. 构建 Ceres 问题 ----
        double pose[6] = {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
        ceres::Problem problem;

        // 2.1 准备“当前帧”用于插值的 double 数组（并行）
        std::vector<double> cur_int(v_res_ * h_res_), cx(v_res_ * h_res_), cy(v_res_ * h_res_), cz(v_res_ * h_res_);
        const int N = v_res_ * h_res_;
// OpenMP 并行化数组拷贝（若未启用 OpenMP，编译器会忽略 pragma）
#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(num_threads_)
        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i)
        {
            cur_int[i] = static_cast<double>(intensity_8u.data[i]); // 8U → double
            cx[i] = px[i];
            cy[i] = py[i];
            cz[i] = pz[i];
        }

        // 2.2 并行构建残差块（不直接向 Problem 写，线程局部收集后合并）
        std::vector<ceres::CostFunction *> costs;
        costs.reserve(N / std::max(1, sample_step_));
        std::vector<ceres::LossFunction *> losses;
        losses.reserve(N / std::max(1, sample_step_));
        int added = 0;

#pragma omp parallel num_threads(num_threads_)
        {
            std::vector<ceres::CostFunction *> local_costs;
            std::vector<ceres::LossFunction *> local_losses;
            int local_added = 0;

// 注意：v 按 1 步长，u 按 sample_step_（与原实现一致）:contentReference[oaicite:5]{index=5}
#pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (int v = 0; v < v_res_; ++v)
            {
                for (int u = 0; u < h_res_; u += sample_step_)
                {
                    const int id = v * h_res_ + u;
                    if (!prev_mask_[id])
                        continue;

                    // 取上一帧像素的强度与3D点（与原代码一致）
                    const double Iprev = static_cast<double>(prev_intensity_.at<uchar>(v, u));
                    const double X = prev_x_[id], Y = prev_y_[id], Z = prev_z_[id];

                    // 为 AutoDiffCostFunction 分配 functor；CostFunction 默认接管所有权
                    auto *functor = new PhotometricGeometricResidual(
                        X, Y, Z, Iprev,
                        cur_int.data(), cx.data(), cy.data(), cz.data(),
                        h_res_, v_res_, min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_,
                        w_photo_, w_geo_);

                    ceres::CostFunction *cost =
                        new ceres::AutoDiffCostFunction<PhotometricGeometricResidual, 4, 6>(functor);
                    ceres::LossFunction *loss = new ceres::HuberLoss(1.0);

                    local_costs.push_back(cost);
                    local_losses.push_back(loss);
                    local_added++;
                }
            }

// 合并到全局容器（减少并发冲突：每线程仅进入一次临界区）
#pragma omp critical
            {
                costs.insert(costs.end(), local_costs.begin(), local_costs.end());
                losses.insert(losses.end(), local_losses.begin(), local_losses.end());
                added += local_added;
            }
        }

        ROS_INFO("帧 %d: 残差块 %d 个", frame_idx_, added);
        if (added < min_matches_)
        {
            ROS_WARN("残差过少，跳过优化");
            // 缓存当前帧并返回（与原流程一致）
            afterSolveAndCache(intensity_8u, px, py, pz, mask);
            return;
        }

        // 2.3 单线程批量加入 Problem（Problem 非线程安全）
        for (size_t i = 0; i < costs.size(); ++i)
        {
            problem.AddResidualBlock(costs[i], losses[i], pose);
        }

        // 2.4 Ceres 求解（多线程）
        ceres::Solver::Options opt;
        opt.linear_solver_type = ceres::DENSE_QR; // DoF=6：DENSE_QR 实测稳定高效
        opt.max_num_iterations = 5;               // 保持原设定
        opt.num_threads = num_threads_;           // 多线程残差/雅可比评估
        // opt.num_linear_solver_threads = num_threads_;           // 线性求解并行（QR/Eigen 内部并行）
        opt.minimizer_progress_to_stdout = false;
        opt.logging_type = ceres::SILENT;

        ceres::Solver::Summary sum;
        auto t_opt_s = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        ceres::Solve(opt, &problem, &sum);
        auto t_opt_e = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        double t_opt = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_opt_e - t_opt_s).count();

        ROS_INFO("优化完成: %.2fms  初始%.4f → 结束%.4f", t_opt, sum.initial_cost, sum.final_cost);
        double tx = pose[3], ty = pose[4], tz = pose[5];
        ROS_INFO("估计平移 [%.4f %.4f %.4f]", tx, ty, tz);

        // ---- 3. 结果保存 & 可视化 ----
        std::ofstream ofs(output_dir_ + "/T_log.txt", std::ios::app);
        ofs << frame_idx_ << " " << tx << " " << ty << " " << tz << "\n";
        ofs.close();
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/frame_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png", intensity_8u);

        // ---- 4. 缓存当前帧，进入下一帧 ----
        afterSolveAndCache(intensity_8u, px, py, pz, mask);

        auto t_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        double total = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_end - t_start).count();
        ROS_INFO("帧耗时 %.1f ms", total);
    }

    // 缓存辅助：与原始逻辑一致，抽出便于复用
    void afterSolveAndCache(const cv::Mat &intensity_8u,
                            const std::vector<double> &px,
                            const std::vector<double> &py,
                            const std::vector<double> &pz,
                            const std::vector<char> &mask)
    {
        prev_intensity_ = intensity_8u.clone();
        prev_x_ = px;
        prev_y_ = py;
        prev_z_ = pz;
        prev_mask_ = mask;
        frame_idx_++;
    }
};

// =============== 主函数 ===============
int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_intensity_dvo_mt");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");
    LidarIntensityDVO dvo(nh);
    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}




// projection_node.cpp
//
// 测试节点：订阅 128x2500 点云，投影为强度图（多线程），保存 PNG。
// 重点是投影部分：利用点云结构：128 线 × 每线 2500 点，按行=线束直接映射。
// 依赖：roscpp, sensor_msgs, OpenCV, (可选)OpenMP。

#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>
#include <chrono>
#include <limits>
#include <cstdio> // std::snprintf

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

// 简单点结构，用来存储解包后的点云
struct SimplePoint128x2500
{
    float x;
    float y;
    float z;
    float intensity;
};

// 投影函数
//  - msg: 点云（假定 128 线 × 2500 点，按线束顺序排列）
//  - output_dir: 输出图片目录（末尾不要加斜杠）
//  - frame_idx: 当前帧编号，用于文件命名
//  - intensity_f: 输出 float 强度图 (CV_32F, 128x2500)
//  - intensity_8u: 输出 8U 强度图 (CV_8U,  128x2500, NORM_MINMAX 归一化)
//  - pixel_x/y/z: 像素 -> 该像素代表的 3D 点坐标
//  - has_point: 标记该像素是否有点（0/1）
//  - t_proj_ms: 投影耗时（毫秒）
// 优化版：不再使用 PointCloud2 迭代器和中间 pts 向量，
// 直接从 msg->data 原始内存中读取 x,y,z,intensity。
void projectLidarToIntensity(
    const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg,
    const std::string &output_dir,
    int frame_idx,
    cv::Mat &intensity_f,
    cv::Mat &intensity_8u,
    std::vector<float> &pixel_x,
    std::vector<float> &pixel_y,
    std::vector<float> &pixel_z,
    std::vector<char> &has_point,
    double &t_proj_ms)
{
    auto t_proj_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    if (!msg || msg->data.empty())
    {
        ROS_WARN("projectLidarToIntensity: empty point cloud.");
        intensity_f.release();
        intensity_8u.release();
        pixel_x.clear();
        pixel_y.clear();
        pixel_z.clear();
        has_point.clear();
        t_proj_ms = 0.0;
        return;
    }

    // 目标图像：128 行（线束），每行最多 2500 列（该线束点序）
    const int lines = 128;
    const int points_per_ln = 2500;
    const int v_res = lines;
    const int h_res = points_per_ln;
    const size_t total_pix = (size_t)v_res * h_res;

    const size_t num_points = (size_t)msg->width * msg->height;

    // ---------- 找到 x/y/z/intensity/ring 在 point_step 里的偏移 ----------
    int offset_x = -1, offset_y = -1, offset_z = -1, offset_i = -1, offset_ring = -1;
    int ring_datatype = -1; // 保存 ring 的类型，假定为 UINT16 最常见
    // offset_ring = msg->fields.begin().base()->datatype;
    for (const auto &f : msg->fields)
    {
        if (f.name == "x")          offset_x = f.offset;
        else if (f.name == "y")     offset_y = f.offset;
        else if (f.name == "z")     offset_z = f.offset;
        else if (f.name == "intensity") offset_i = f.offset;
        else if (f.name == "ring")  { offset_ring = f.offset; ring_datatype = f.datatype; }
    }

    if (offset_x < 0 || offset_y < 0 || offset_z < 0 || offset_i < 0 || offset_ring < 0)
    {
        ROS_ERROR("projectLidarToIntensity: x/y/z/intensity/ring fields not all found.");
        t_proj_ms = 0.0;
        return;
    }

    if (ring_datatype != sensor_msgs::PointField::UINT16 &&
        ring_datatype != sensor_msgs::PointField::INT16)
    {
        ROS_WARN_THROTTLE(1.0,
            "projectLidarToIntensity: ring field datatype is %d, expected (U)INT16. "
            "Code assumes 16-bit ring.", ring_datatype);
    }

    const uint8_t *base_ptr = msg->data.data();
    const size_t point_step = msg->point_step;
    auto t_ring_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    // ---------- 第 1 步：单线程按 ring 分桶 ----------
    std::vector<std::vector<size_t>> ring_indices(lines);
    for (int r = 0; r < lines; ++r)
        ring_indices[r].reserve(points_per_ln);

    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < num_points; ++idx)
    {
        const uint8_t *p_base = base_ptr + idx * point_step;
        // 读取 ring（假定 16bit）
        const uint16_t *pr = reinterpret_cast<const uint16_t *>(p_base + offset_ring);
        int ring = (int)(*pr);

        if (ring < 0 || ring >= lines)
            continue;

        // 最多记录 points_per_ln 个索引，多余的忽略（不影响图像尺寸）
        if ((int)ring_indices[ring].size() < points_per_ln)
            ring_indices[ring].push_back(idx);
    }
    auto t_ring_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    double t_ring_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_ring_end - t_ring_start).count();
    std::cout << "ring_ms:" << t_ring_ms << "\n"
              << std::endl;
    // ---------- 第 2 步：初始化输出图像和 3D 映射 ----------
    intensity_f = cv::Mat(v_res, h_res, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
    pixel_x.assign(total_pix, 0.0f);
    pixel_y.assign(total_pix, 0.0f);
    pixel_z.assign(total_pix, 0.0f);
    has_point.assign(total_pix, 0);
    auto t_turn_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    // ---------- 第 3 步：多线程，按 ring(行) 填图 ----------
    int omp_threads = 1;
#ifdef _OPENMP
    omp_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
#endif

#ifdef _OPENMP
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(omp_threads)
#endif

    for (int line = 0; line < lines; ++line)
    {
        if (line >= v_res)
            continue;

        float *row_ptr = intensity_f.ptr<float>(line);
        auto &idx_vec = ring_indices[line];

        const int max_col = std::min((int)idx_vec.size(), points_per_ln);

        for (int col = 0; col < max_col; ++col)
        {
            size_t idx = idx_vec[(size_t)col];
            if (idx >= num_points)
                continue;

            const uint8_t *p_base = base_ptr + idx * point_step;

            const float *px = reinterpret_cast<const float *>(p_base + offset_x);
            const float *py = reinterpret_cast<const float *>(p_base + offset_y);
            const float *pz = reinterpret_cast<const float *>(p_base + offset_z);
            const float *pi = reinterpret_cast<const float *>(p_base + offset_i);

            float x = *px;
            float y = *py;
            float z = *pz;
            float intensity = *pi;

            // 跳过“零点”或无效强度
            if (!std::isfinite(x) || !std::isfinite(y) || !std::isfinite(z) ||
                !std::isfinite(intensity) || intensity <= 0.0f ||
                (x == 0.0f && y == 0.0f && z == 0.0f))
            {
                continue;
            }

            const int u = col;  // 列：该线束内的顺序
            const int v = line; // 行：线束编号 (= ring)

            // 安全检查
            if (u < 0 || u >= h_res)
                continue;
            if (v < 0 || v >= v_res)
                continue;

            const size_t pid = (size_t)v * h_res + (size_t)u;

            // 若同一像素被多次命中，取最大强度
            if (!has_point[pid] || intensity > row_ptr[u])
            {
                row_ptr[u] = intensity;
                pixel_x[pid] = x;
                pixel_y[pid] = y;
                pixel_z[pid] = z;
                has_point[pid] = 1;
            }
        }
    }
    std::cout << "threads:" << omp_threads << std::endl;
    auto t_turn_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    double t_turn_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_turn_end - t_turn_start).count();
    std::cout << "turn_ms:" << t_turn_ms << "\n"
              << std::endl;
    // ---------- 第 4 步：归一化到 0~255 并输出 8U 图像 ----------
    cv::normalize(intensity_f, intensity_8u, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
    auto t_proj_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    char fname[256];
    std::snprintf(fname, sizeof(fname), "/intensity_ring_%06d.png", frame_idx);
    std::string path = output_dir + std::string(fname);
    if (!cv::imwrite(path, intensity_8u))
    {
        ROS_WARN("Failed to write intensity image: %s", path.c_str());
    }

    // ---------- 第 5 步：耗时 ----------

    t_proj_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_proj_end - t_proj_start).count();

    ROS_INFO("[Frame %d] projection (ring-based, MT) time = %.3f ms, saved: %s",
             frame_idx, t_proj_ms, path.c_str());
}

// 一个简单的测试节点：订阅点云、调用投影函数
class ProjectionTester
{
public:
    ProjectionTester(ros::NodeHandle &nh)
        : nh_(nh), frame_idx_(0)
    {
        nh_.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, std::string("/home/lb/Piont_cloudToImage/lidar_output"));
        nh_.param<std::string>("cloud_topic", cloud_topic_, std::string("/lidar_points"));

        // 创建输出目录（简单处理：让系统自己建父目录，或者你自己预先 mkdir）
        ROS_INFO("ProjectionTester: output_dir=%s, cloud_topic=%s",
                 output_dir_.c_str(), cloud_topic_.c_str());

        sub_ = nh_.subscribe(cloud_topic_, 1, &ProjectionTester::cloudCallback, this);
    }

private:
    void cloudCallback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg)
    {
        cv::Mat intensity_f, intensity_8u;
        std::vector<float> px, py, pz;
        std::vector<char> has_point;
        double t_ms = 0.0;

        projectLidarToIntensity(msg, output_dir_, frame_idx_,
                                intensity_f, intensity_8u,
                                px, py, pz, has_point,
                                t_ms);

        frame_idx_++;
    }

    ros::NodeHandle nh_;
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    std::string output_dir_;
    std::string cloud_topic_;
    int frame_idx_;
};

int main(int argc, char **argv)
{
    ros::init(argc, argv, "lidar_projection_128x2500_test_node");
    ros::NodeHandle nh("~");

    ProjectionTester node(nh);

    ros::spin();
    return 0;
}



// lidar_intensity_orb_match_full_ransac3d.cpp
//
// 功能：
//  - 将 LiDAR 点云按角度或 ring 投影为强度图（v_res x h_res），使用“最大强度”融合。
//  - 保存 raw/enhanced/orb_vis 图像。
//  - 提取 ORB，knn(Hamming) + Ratio Test + Hamming filter。
//  - 2D-RANSAC（可开关）筛除图像伪匹配，并保存 match_2d_<frame>.png。
//  - 2D 内点映射为 3D 对，3D-RANSAC（支持并行）剔除空间外点，保存 match_3d_<frame>.png 与 matches_3d_<frame>.txt。
//  - 基于 3D 内点用 SVD 求刚体变换并取逆得到车体位姿增量，保存 all_Tguess.txt（frame x y z roll pitch yaw）
//  - 每帧输出并写入 time_log.csv（各阶段耗时 ms）
//  - 详细注释，便于调试与扩展；在无 OpenMP 时自动回退单线程。
//
// 使用说明：
//  - 将此文件加入 catkin 包，编译后运行，确保 topic /lidar_points 发布 sensor_msgs/PointCloud2，且包含字段 x,y,z,intensity（可选 ring）。
//  - 通过 ROS 参数调节图片分辨率、RANSAC/ORB/并行参数、投影模式（spherical|ring）等。

#include <ros/ros.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/PointCloud2.h>
#include <sensor_msgs/point_cloud2_iterator.h>

#include <opencv2/opencv.hpp>
#include <opencv2/features2d.hpp>

#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <errno.h>

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cmath>
#include <chrono>
#include <random>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <limits>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

template <typename T>
inline T clampValue(T v, T lo, T hi)
{
    return (v < lo) ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v);
}

inline double dist3D2(const cv::Point3f &a, const cv::Point3f &b)
{
    double dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
    return dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz;
}

class LidarIntensityORBMatchFullRANSAC3D
{
public:
    LidarIntensityORBMatchFullRANSAC3D(ros::NodeHandle &nh)
        : nh_(nh), frame_idx_(0), has_prev_frame_(false)
    {
        // ---------------- 参数读取（可通过 launch/rosparam 调整） ----------------
        nh_.param<std::string>("output_dir", output_dir_, std::string("./lidar_output"));
        nh_.param<int>("v_res", v_res_, 128);              // 垂直分辨率（行数）
        nh_.param<int>("h_res", h_res_, 500);              // 水平分辨率（列数）
        nh_.param<double>("h_fov_deg", h_fov_deg_, 120.0); // 水平 FoV
        nh_.param<double>("v_min_deg", v_min_deg_, -12.5); // 垂直最小角
        nh_.param<double>("v_max_deg", v_max_deg_, 12.9);  // 垂直最大角
        nh_.param<int>("sample_step", sample_step_, 1);    // 点云采样步长（加速）

        nh_.param<float>("ratio_thresh", ratio_thresh_, 0.75f);        // Lowe 比率
        nh_.param<int>("hamming_thresh", hamming_thresh_, 50);         // Hamming 上限
        nh_.param<double>("ransac_2d_reproj", ransac_2d_reproj_, 3.0); // 2D ransac 重投影阈 (px)
        nh_.param<double>("ransac_3d_thresh", ransac_3d_thresh_, 0.2); // 3D ransac 阈 (m)
        nh_.param<int>("ransac_3d_iters", ransac_3d_iters_, 100);      // 3D ransac 迭代次数

        nh_.param<bool>("enable_blur", enable_blur_, true);
        nh_.param<std::string>("contrast_mode", contrast_mode_, std::string("clahe")); // none|equalize|clahe
        nh_.param<int>("orb_vis_radius", orb_vis_radius_, 2);

        // ORB 参数
        nh_.param<int>("orb_nfeatures", orb_nfeatures_, 700);
        nh_.param<double>("orb_scaleFactor", orb_scaleFactor_, 1.1);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_nlevels", orb_nlevels_, 4);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_edgeThreshold", orb_edgeThreshold_, 5);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_patchSize", orb_patchSize_, 31);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_fastThreshold", orb_fastThreshold_, 5);
        nh_.param<int>("orb_wta_k", orb_wta_k_, 2);
        int orb_score_type_int = 0;
        nh_.param<int>("orb_score_type", orb_score_type_int, 0);
        orb_score_type_ = (orb_score_type_int == 1) ? cv::ORB::FAST_SCORE : cv::ORB::HARRIS_SCORE;

        // 新增：行为/并行开关参数
        nh_.param<bool>("enable_ransac_2d", enable_ransac_2d_, true);                          // 是否使用 2D RANSAC
        nh_.param<bool>("enable_multithread", enable_multithread_, true);                      // 是否启用 OpenCV 内部多线程
        nh_.param<int>("num_threads", num_threads_, 26);                                        // OpenCV 线程数（同时也作为默认 OMP 线程数）
        nh_.param<bool>("enable_parallel_projection", enable_parallel_projection_, true);      // 投影并行
        nh_.param<bool>("enable_parallel_ransac3d", enable_parallel_ransac3d_, true);          // 3D-RANSAC 并行
        nh_.param<int>("ransac3d_threads", ransac3d_threads_, num_threads_);                   // 3D-RANSAC 并行线程数
        nh_.param<std::string>("projection_mode", projection_mode_, std::string("ring")); // spherical|ring
        nh_.param<int>("ring_count", ring_count_, 0);                                          // ring 模式的行数（0 表示不强制）

        // 创建输出目录（如果不存在）
        if (mkdir(output_dir_.c_str(), 0777) && errno != EEXIST)
        {
            ROS_WARN("mkdir failed or already exists: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        }

        // 角度边界（弧度）
        min_az_ = -h_fov_deg_ / 2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_az_ = h_fov_deg_ / 2.0 * M_PI / 180.0;
        min_el_ = v_min_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;
        max_el_ = v_max_deg_ * M_PI / 180.0;

        // 订阅点云话题（默认 /lidar_points）
        sub_ = nh_.subscribe("/lidar_points", 1, &LidarIntensityORBMatchFullRANSAC3D::callback, this);

        // 初始化随机数发生器
        rng_.seed(std::random_device{}());

        // OpenCV 内部多线程设置
        if (enable_multithread_)
        {
            cv::setUseOptimized(true);
            cv::setNumThreads(num_threads_);
            ROS_INFO("OpenCV multithreading enabled. num_threads=%d", num_threads_);
        }
        else
        {
            cv::setNumThreads(1);
            ROS_INFO("OpenCV multithreading disabled, force num_threads=1");
        }

        // 准备 time_log.csv 文件表头（追加模式）
        std::ofstream tlog(output_dir_ + "/time_log.csv", std::ios::app);
        if (tlog.tellp() == 0)
        { // file is empty -> write header
            tlog << "frame,proj_ms,enh_ms,orb_ms,match_ms,ransac2d_ms,ransac3d_ms,total_ms\n";
        }
        tlog.close();

        ROS_INFO("Node initialized. Output dir: %s", output_dir_.c_str());
        ROS_INFO("Image size: %d x %d ; vertical range: %.2f ~ %.2f deg", v_res_, h_res_, v_min_deg_, v_max_deg_);
        ROS_INFO("2D RANSAC: %s, projection: %s, parallel proj: %s, parallel 3D-RANSAC: %s",
                 enable_ransac_2d_ ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED",
                 projection_mode_.c_str(),
                 enable_parallel_projection_ ? "ON" : "OFF",
                 enable_parallel_ransac3d_ ? "ON" : "OFF");
    }

private:
    // 每个像素的代表点（使用最大强度点作为代表）
    struct PixAcc
    {
        float max_val = 0.0f;
        float max_x = 0.0f, max_y = 0.0f, max_z = 0.0f;
        bool has_point = false;
    };

    // 为并行投影解包用的简易点结构
    struct SimplePoint
    {
        float x, y, z, i;
    };

    // 成员变量（参数、缓存等）
    ros::NodeHandle nh_;
    ros::Subscriber sub_;
    std::string output_dir_;
    int v_res_, h_res_, sample_step_;
    double h_fov_deg_, v_min_deg_, v_max_deg_;
    double min_az_, max_az_, min_el_, max_el_;

    float ratio_thresh_;
    int hamming_thresh_;
    double ransac_2d_reproj_;
    double ransac_3d_thresh_;
    int ransac_3d_iters_;

    bool enable_blur_;
    std::string contrast_mode_;
    int orb_vis_radius_;

    int orb_nfeatures_, orb_nlevels_, orb_edgeThreshold_, orb_patchSize_, orb_fastThreshold_, orb_wta_k_;
    double orb_scaleFactor_;
    cv::ORB::ScoreType orb_score_type_;

    // 行为/并行开关
    bool enable_ransac_2d_;
    bool enable_multithread_;
    int num_threads_;
    bool enable_parallel_projection_;
    bool enable_parallel_ransac3d_;
    int ransac3d_threads_;
    std::string projection_mode_;
    int ring_count_;

    // cache & state
    int frame_idx_;
    bool has_prev_frame_;
    cv::Mat prev_img_; // store enhanced image for visualization
    cv::Mat prev_desc_;
    std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> prev_kp_;
    std::vector<float> prev_px_, prev_py_, prev_pz_; // pixel -> 3D representative
    std::vector<char> prev_has_;
    std::mt19937 rng_;

    // ------------------ 辅助函数 ------------------

    // 解包点云到连续数组（便于并行）
    void unpackPointCloud(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg,
                          std::vector<SimplePoint> &pts)
    {
        pts.clear();
        pts.reserve((size_t)msg->width * msg->height);
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_i(*msg, "intensity");
        int idx_iter = 0;
        for (; it_x != it_x.end(); ++it_x, ++it_y, ++it_z, ++it_i, ++idx_iter)
        {
            if (sample_step_ > 1 && (idx_iter % sample_step_ != 0))
                continue;
            float x = *it_x, y = *it_y, z = *it_z, in = *it_i;
            if (!std::isfinite(x) || !std::isfinite(y) || !std::isfinite(z) || !std::isfinite(in))
                continue;
            pts.push_back({x, y, z, in});
        }
    }

    inline int pixelUFromAz(double az) const
    {
        double uf = (az - min_az_) / (max_az_ - min_az_) * (h_res_ - 1);
        return clampValue((int)std::round(uf), 0, h_res_ - 1);
    }
    inline int pixelVFromEl(double el) const
    {
        double vf = (max_el_ - el) / (max_el_ - min_el_) * (v_res_ - 1);
        return clampValue((int)std::round(vf), 0, v_res_ - 1);
    }

    // 是否存在 ring 字段
    bool hasRingField(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg) const
    {
        for (const auto &f : msg->fields)
            if (f.name == "ring")
                return true;
        return false;
    }

    // 并行：球面投影（最大强度融合，线程本地 grid + 归并）
    void buildIntensityImageSphericalParallel(
        const std::vector<SimplePoint> &pts,
        cv::Mat &intensity_f,
        std::vector<float> &pixel_x, std::vector<float> &pixel_y,
        std::vector<float> &pixel_z, std::vector<char> &has_point)
    {
        const size_t cells = (size_t)v_res_ * h_res_;
        std::vector<PixAcc> global_grid(cells);
        int T = (enable_parallel_projection_ && enable_multithread_ && num_threads_ > 1) ? num_threads_ : 1;

        // 线程本地 grids
        std::vector<std::vector<PixAcc>> tgrids((size_t)T, std::vector<PixAcc>(cells));

// 并行分块处理点
#ifdef _OPENMP
#pragma omp parallel num_threads(T)
#endif
        {
            int tid = 0;
#ifdef _OPENMP
            tid = omp_get_thread_num();
#endif
            auto &g = tgrids[(size_t)tid];

#ifdef _OPENMP
#pragma omp for schedule(static)
#endif
            for (int i = 0; i < (int)pts.size(); ++i)
            {
                const auto &p = pts[(size_t)i];
                double az = atan2((double)p.y, (double)p.x);
                double el = atan2((double)p.z, std::sqrt((double)p.x * (double)p.x + (double)p.y * (double)p.y));
                if (az < min_az_ || az > max_az_ || el < min_el_ || el > max_el_)
                    continue;

                int u = pixelUFromAz(az);
                int v = pixelVFromEl(el);
                size_t pid = (size_t)v * h_res_ + (size_t)u;
                PixAcc &cell = g[pid];
                if (!cell.has_point || p.i > cell.max_val)
                {
                    cell.max_val = p.i;
                    cell.max_x = p.x;
                    cell.max_y = p.y;
                    cell.max_z = p.z;
                    cell.has_point = true;
                }
            }
        }

        // 归并：取最大强度
        for (int t = 0; t < T; ++t)
        {
            const auto &tg = tgrids[(size_t)t];
            for (size_t id = 0; id < cells; ++id)
            {
                const PixAcc &src = tg[id];
                PixAcc &dst = global_grid[id];
                if (src.has_point && (!dst.has_point || src.max_val > dst.max_val))
                    dst = src;
            }
        }

        // 输出矩阵和映射
        intensity_f = cv::Mat(v_res_, h_res_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
        pixel_x.assign(cells, 0.0f);
        pixel_y.assign(cells, 0.0f);
        pixel_z.assign(cells, 0.0f);
        has_point.assign(cells, 0);

        for (size_t id = 0; id < cells; ++id)
        {
            const PixAcc &cell = global_grid[id];
            if (cell.has_point)
            {
                int r = (int)(id / h_res_), c = (int)(id % h_res_);
                intensity_f.at<float>(r, c) = cell.max_val;
                pixel_x[id] = cell.max_x;
                pixel_y[id] = cell.max_y;
                pixel_z[id] = cell.max_z;
                has_point[id] = 1;
            }
        }
    }

    // 可选：ring 投影（若有 "ring" 字段则严格按线束行，否则退化为按垂直角近似）
    void buildIntensityImageRing(
        const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg,
        cv::Mat &intensity_f,
        std::vector<float> &pixel_x, std::vector<float> &pixel_y,
        std::vector<float> &pixel_z, std::vector<char> &has_point)
    {
        const size_t cells = (size_t)v_res_ * h_res_;
        std::vector<PixAcc> grid(cells);

        bool has_ring = hasRingField(msg);
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_x(*msg, "x");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_y(*msg, "y");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_z(*msg, "z");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<float> it_i(*msg, "intensity");
        sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t> it_ring(*msg, has_ring ? "ring" : "x"); // 占位

        int idx_iter = 0, max_ring_seen = -1;
        for (; it_x != it_x.end(); ++it_x, ++it_y, ++it_z, ++it_i, ++idx_iter)
        {
            if (sample_step_ > 1 && (idx_iter % sample_step_ != 0))
                continue;
            float x = *it_x, y = *it_y, z = *it_z, in = *it_i;
            if (!std::isfinite(x) || !std::isfinite(y) || !std::isfinite(z) || !std::isfinite(in))
                continue;

            int ring = 0;
            if (has_ring)
            {
                if (idx_iter == 0)
                    it_ring = sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstIterator<uint16_t>(*msg, "ring");
                else
                    ++it_ring;
                ring = (int)*it_ring;
            }
            else
            {
                double el = atan2((double)z, std::sqrt((double)x * (double)x + (double)y * (double)y));
                ring = pixelVFromEl(el);  // 使用已有量化
                ring = v_res_ - 1 - ring; // 可按需求翻转
            }
            max_ring_seen = std::max(max_ring_seen, ring);
            if (ring < 0 || ring >= v_res_)
                continue;

            double az = atan2((double)y, (double)x);
            if (az < min_az_ || az > max_az_)
                continue;
            int u = pixelUFromAz(az);
            size_t pid = (size_t)ring * h_res_ + (size_t)u;

            PixAcc &cell = grid[pid];
            if (!cell.has_point || in > cell.max_val)
            {
                cell.max_val = in;
                cell.max_x = x;
                cell.max_y = y;
                cell.max_z = z;
                cell.has_point = true;
            }
        }

        if (ring_count_ == 0 && max_ring_seen >= 0)
            ROS_INFO_THROTTLE(1.0, "Detected ring up to %d. Consider setting ring_count=%d and v_res accordingly.", max_ring_seen, max_ring_seen + 1);

        // 输出
        intensity_f = cv::Mat(v_res_, h_res_, CV_32F, cv::Scalar(0.0f));
        pixel_x.assign(cells, 0.0f);
        pixel_y.assign(cells, 0.0f);
        pixel_z.assign(cells, 0.0f);
        has_point.assign(cells, 0);

        for (size_t id = 0; id < cells; ++id)
        {
            const PixAcc &cell = grid[id];
            if (cell.has_point)
            {
                int r = (int)(id / h_res_), c = (int)(id % h_res_);
                intensity_f.at<float>(r, c) = cell.max_val;
                pixel_x[id] = cell.max_x;
                pixel_y[id] = cell.max_y;
                pixel_z[id] = cell.max_z;
                has_point[id] = 1;
            }
        }
    }

    // SVD 求解刚体变换（P -> Q）
    bool estimateRigidSVD(const std::vector<cv::Point3f> &P,
                          const std::vector<cv::Point3f> &Q,
                          cv::Mat &R, cv::Mat &t)
    {
        if (P.size() < 3)
            return false;
        cv::Point3d meanP(0, 0, 0), meanQ(0, 0, 0);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < P.size(); ++i)
        {
            meanP += cv::Point3d(P[i].x, P[i].y, P[i].z);
            meanQ += cv::Point3d(Q[i].x, Q[i].y, Q[i].z);
        }
        meanP *= 1.0 / P.size();
        meanQ *= 1.0 / P.size();

        cv::Mat H = cv::Mat::zeros(3, 3, CV_64F);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < P.size(); ++i)
        {
            cv::Mat p = (cv::Mat_<double>(3, 1) << P[i].x - meanP.x, P[i].y - meanP.y, P[i].z - meanP.z);
            cv::Mat q = (cv::Mat_<double>(1, 3) << Q[i].x - meanQ.x, Q[i].y - meanQ.y, Q[i].z - meanQ.z);
            H += p * q;
        }
        cv::Mat U, S, Vt;
        cv::SVD::compute(H, S, U, Vt);
        R = Vt.t() * U.t();
        if (cv::determinant(R) < 0)
        {
            Vt.row(2) *= -1;
            R = Vt.t() * U.t();
        }
        cv::Mat meanP_m = (cv::Mat_<double>(3, 1) << meanP.x, meanP.y, meanP.z);
        cv::Mat meanQ_m = (cv::Mat_<double>(3, 1) << meanQ.x, meanQ.y, meanQ.z);
        t = meanQ_m - R * meanP_m;
        return true;
    }

    // 快速应用 R、t（避免频繁构造小矩阵）
    inline void applyRT(const cv::Mat &R, const cv::Mat &t,
                        const cv::Point3f &p, cv::Point3f &q) const
    {
        const double *r = (const double *)R.data;
        const double *tv = (const double *)t.data;
        double x = p.x, y = p.y, z = p.z;
        q.x = (float)(r[0] * x + r[1] * y + r[2] * z + tv[0]);
        q.y = (float)(r[3] * x + r[4] * y + r[5] * z + tv[1]);
        q.z = (float)(r[6] * x + r[7] * y + r[8] * z + tv[2]);
    }

    // 3D-RANSAC（顺序版，已优化内环）
    std::vector<int> ransac3D(const std::vector<cv::Point3f> &P,
                              const std::vector<cv::Point3f> &Q,
                              double thresh, int iters)
    {
        std::vector<int> best_inliers;
        if (P.size() < 3)
            return best_inliers;
        std::uniform_int_distribution<int> uni(0, (int)P.size() - 1);

        for (int it = 0; it < iters; ++it)
        {
            int a = uni(rng_), b = uni(rng_), c = uni(rng_);
            if (a == b || a == c || b == c)
            {
                --it;
                continue;
            }

            std::vector<cv::Point3f> Ps = {P[a], P[b], P[c]};
            std::vector<cv::Point3f> Qs = {Q[a], Q[b], Q[c]};
            cv::Mat Rtmp, ttmp;
            if (!estimateRigidSVD(Ps, Qs, Rtmp, ttmp))
                continue;

            std::vector<int> inliers;
            inliers.reserve(P.size());
            for (size_t i = 0; i < P.size(); ++i)
            {
                cv::Point3f q_est;
                applyRT(Rtmp, ttmp, P[i], q_est);
                if (std::sqrt(dist3D2(q_est, Q[i])) < thresh)
                    inliers.push_back((int)i);
            }
            if (inliers.size() > best_inliers.size())
                best_inliers.swap(inliers);
        }

        ROS_INFO("3D-RANSAC result: kept %zu / %zu (%.1f%%)",
                 best_inliers.size(), P.size(),
                 100.0 * best_inliers.size() / std::max<size_t>(1, P.size()));
        return best_inliers;
    }

    // 3D-RANSAC（并行版，外层假设并行 + 线程本地最优）
    std::vector<int> ransac3D_parallel(const std::vector<cv::Point3f> &P,
                                       const std::vector<cv::Point3f> &Q,
                                       double thresh, int iters, int threads)
    {
        std::vector<int> best_global;
        if (P.size() < 3)
            return best_global;
        const int N = (int)P.size();
        threads = std::max(1, threads);

#ifdef _OPENMP
#pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
#endif
        {
            // 线程本地 RNG
            std::mt19937 rng_local((uint32_t)(0x9e3779b1u ^ (frame_idx_ * 1315423911u)));
#ifdef _OPENMP
            rng_local.discard((uint32_t)omp_get_thread_num() * 7);
#endif
            std::uniform_int_distribution<int> uni(0, N - 1);

            std::vector<int> best_local;

#ifdef _OPENMP
#pragma omp for schedule(static)
#endif
            for (int it = 0; it < iters; ++it)
            {
                int a = uni(rng_local), b = uni(rng_local), c = uni(rng_local);
                if (a == b || a == c || b == c)
                {
                    --it;
                    continue;
                }

                std::vector<cv::Point3f> Ps = {P[a], P[b], P[c]};
                std::vector<cv::Point3f> Qs = {Q[a], Q[b], Q[c]};
                cv::Mat Rtmp, ttmp;
                if (!estimateRigidSVD(Ps, Qs, Rtmp, ttmp))
                    continue;

                std::vector<int> inliers;
                inliers.reserve(N);
                for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i)
                {
                    cv::Point3f q_est;
                    applyRT(Rtmp, ttmp, P[i], q_est);
                    float dx = q_est.x - Q[i].x;
                    float dy = q_est.y - Q[i].y;
                    float dz = q_est.z - Q[i].z;
                    if (std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) < (float)thresh)
                        inliers.push_back(i);
                }
                if (inliers.size() > best_local.size())
                    best_local.swap(inliers);
            }

// 归并全局最优
#ifdef _OPENMP
#pragma omp critical
#endif
            {
                if (best_local.size() > best_global.size())
                    best_global.swap(best_local);
            }
        }

        ROS_INFO("3D-RANSAC(parallel) result: kept %zu / %zu (%.1f%%)",
                 best_global.size(), P.size(),
                 100.0 * best_global.size() / std::max<size_t>(1, P.size()));
        return best_global;
    }

    // 将两帧图像上下拼接并画出 matches（matches 为 prev->cur 的匹配），保存 path
    void visualizeMatchesStacked(const cv::Mat &prev_img, const cv::Mat &cur_img,
                                 const std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> &prev_kp,
                                 const std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> &cur_kp,
                                 const std::vector<cv::DMatch> &matches,
                                 const std::string &path)
    {
        cv::Mat prev_color, cur_color;
        cv::cvtColor(prev_img, prev_color, cv::COLOR_GRAY2BGR);
        cv::cvtColor(cur_img, cur_color, cv::COLOR_GRAY2BGR);

        int w = std::max(prev_color.cols, cur_color.cols);
        int h = prev_color.rows + cur_color.rows;
        cv::Mat canvas(h, w, CV_8UC3, cv::Scalar(0, 0, 0));
        prev_color.copyTo(canvas(cv::Rect(0, 0, prev_color.cols, prev_color.rows)));
        cur_color.copyTo(canvas(cv::Rect(0, prev_color.rows, cur_color.cols, cur_color.rows)));

        // 随机颜色种子（固定可复现）
        std::mt19937 rng_local(frame_idx_);
        std::uniform_int_distribution<int> ud(0, 255);

        for (const auto &m : matches)
        {
            cv::Point2f a = prev_kp[m.queryIdx].pt;
            cv::Point2f b = cur_kp[m.trainIdx].pt + cv::Point2f(0.0f, (float)prev_color.rows);
            cv::Scalar color(ud(rng_local), ud(rng_local), ud(rng_local));
            cv::circle(canvas, a, 2, color, -1);
            cv::circle(canvas, b, 2, color, -1);
            cv::line(canvas, a, b, color, 1, cv::LINE_AA);
        }
        cv::imwrite(path, canvas);
    }

    // ------------------ 主回调（每帧） ------------------
    void callback(const sensor_msgs::PointCloud2ConstPtr &msg)
    {
        if (!msg || msg->data.empty())
            return;

        auto t_total_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // ---------- 1) 投影：点云 -> 强度图 ----------
        auto t_proj_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        cv::Mat intensity_f;
        std::vector<float> pixel_x, pixel_y, pixel_z;
        std::vector<char> has_point;

        if (projection_mode_ == "ring")
        {
            buildIntensityImageRing(msg, intensity_f, pixel_x, pixel_y, pixel_z, has_point);
        }
        else
        {
            std::vector<SimplePoint> pts;
            unpackPointCloud(msg, pts);
            buildIntensityImageSphericalParallel(pts, intensity_f, pixel_x, pixel_y, pixel_z, has_point);
        }

        // raw 8U image (normalized) 保存
        cv::Mat intensity_raw_8u;
        cv::normalize(intensity_f, intensity_raw_8u, 0, 255, cv::NORM_MINMAX, CV_8U);
        auto t_proj_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        std::string raw_path = output_dir_ + "/intensity_raw_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png";
        cv::imwrite(raw_path, intensity_raw_8u);

    

        // ---------- 2) 图像增强（CLAHE/Equalize + GaussianBlur） ----------
        auto t_enh_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        cv::Mat intensity_enh = intensity_raw_8u.clone();
        if (contrast_mode_ == "clahe")
        {
            cv::Ptr<cv::CLAHE> clahe = cv::createCLAHE(3.0, cv::Size(8, 8));
            clahe->apply(intensity_raw_8u, intensity_enh);
        }
        else if (contrast_mode_ == "equalize")
        {
            cv::equalizeHist(intensity_raw_8u, intensity_enh);
        }
        if (enable_blur_)
        {
            cv::GaussianBlur(intensity_enh, intensity_enh, cv::Size(3, 3), 0);
        }

        std::string enh_path = output_dir_ + "/intensity_enh_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png";
        cv::imwrite(enh_path, intensity_enh);

        auto t_enh_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // ---------- 3) ORB 特征提取 ----------
        auto t_orb_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        cv::Ptr<cv::ORB> orb = cv::ORB::create(orb_nfeatures_, (float)orb_scaleFactor_, orb_nlevels_,
                                               orb_edgeThreshold_, 0, orb_wta_k_, orb_score_type_,
                                               orb_patchSize_, orb_fastThreshold_);
        std::vector<cv::KeyPoint> keypoints;
        cv::Mat descriptors;
        orb->detectAndCompute(intensity_enh, cv::noArray(), keypoints, descriptors);

        // ORB 可视化（小点）
        cv::Mat orb_vis;
        cv::cvtColor(intensity_enh, orb_vis, cv::COLOR_GRAY2BGR);
        for (const auto &kp : keypoints)
        {
            cv::circle(orb_vis, kp.pt, orb_vis_radius_, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), -1);
        }
        cv::imwrite(output_dir_ + "/orb_vis_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png", orb_vis);

        auto t_orb_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        // variables for timing and counts
        double t_proj_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_proj_end - t_proj_start).count();
        double t_enh_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_enh_end - t_enh_start).count();
        double t_orb_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_orb_end - t_orb_start).count();

        // ---------- 4) 如果有上一帧：做匹配 -> 2D-RANSAC -> 3D-RANSAC -> 保存可视化/结果 ----------
        double t_match_ms = 0.0, t_ransac2d_ms = 0.0, t_ransac3d_ms = 0.0;
        size_t count_2d_inliers = 0, count_3d_inliers = 0;

        if (has_prev_frame_ && !descriptors.empty() && !prev_desc_.empty())
        {
            auto t_match_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

            // KNN match (k=2)
            cv::BFMatcher matcher(cv::NORM_HAMMING);
            std::vector<std::vector<cv::DMatch>> knn_matches;
            matcher.knnMatch(prev_desc_, descriptors, knn_matches, 2);

            // Ratio test (Lowe)
            std::vector<cv::DMatch> ratio_pass;
            ratio_pass.reserve(knn_matches.size());
            for (const auto &m : knn_matches)
            {
                if (m.size() >= 2)
                {
                    if (m[0].distance < ratio_thresh_ * m[1].distance)
                        ratio_pass.push_back(m[0]);
                }
            }

            // Hamming distance filter
            std::vector<cv::DMatch> dist_pass;
            dist_pass.reserve(ratio_pass.size());
            for (const auto &m : ratio_pass)
            {
                if (m.distance <= hamming_thresh_)
                    dist_pass.push_back(m);
            }

            auto t_match_end1 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
            t_match_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_match_end1 - t_match_start).count();

            // --- 2D 阶段：可选 RANSAC ---
            std::vector<cv::Point2f> pts_prev, pts_cur;
            pts_prev.reserve(dist_pass.size());
            pts_cur.reserve(dist_pass.size());
            for (const auto &m : dist_pass)
            {
                pts_prev.push_back(prev_kp_[m.queryIdx].pt);
                pts_cur.push_back(keypoints[m.trainIdx].pt);
            }

            std::vector<cv::DMatch> matches_2d_inliers;

            if (enable_ransac_2d_ && pts_prev.size() >= 8)
            {
                auto t_r2_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

                std::vector<uchar> mask2d;
                cv::Mat H = cv::findHomography(
                    pts_prev, pts_cur,
                    cv::RANSAC,
                    ransac_2d_reproj_,
                    mask2d);
                (void)H; // H may be empty sometimes, mask is used

                // collect 2D inlier matches
                matches_2d_inliers.reserve(dist_pass.size());
                for (size_t i = 0; i < dist_pass.size(); ++i)
                {
                    if (i < mask2d.size() && mask2d[i])
                        matches_2d_inliers.push_back(dist_pass[i]);
                }

                auto t_r2_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
                t_ransac2d_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_r2_end - t_r2_start).count();
            }
            else
            {
                // 不使用 2D RANSAC：直接把 Hamming+ratio 通过的当作内点
                matches_2d_inliers = dist_pass;
                t_ransac2d_ms = 0.0;
            }

            count_2d_inliers = matches_2d_inliers.size();

            // 保存 2D 可视化（上半 prev_enh，下半 cur_enh）
            std::string vis2d_path = output_dir_ + "/match_2d_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png";
            visualizeMatchesStacked(prev_img_, intensity_enh, prev_kp_, keypoints, matches_2d_inliers, vis2d_path);
            ROS_INFO("Saved 2D visualization: %s (inliers=%zu)", vis2d_path.c_str(), matches_2d_inliers.size());

            // --- map 2D inliers -> 3D pairs (P,Q) using prev_px_/pixel_x tables ---
            std::vector<cv::Point3f> P_all, Q_all;  // aligned vectors
            std::vector<cv::DMatch> matches_for_3d; // same order
            P_all.reserve(matches_2d_inliers.size());
            Q_all.reserve(matches_2d_inliers.size());
            for (const auto &m : matches_2d_inliers)
            {
                int u1 = clampValue((int)std::round(prev_kp_[m.queryIdx].pt.x), 0, h_res_ - 1);
                int v1 = clampValue((int)std::round(prev_kp_[m.queryIdx].pt.y), 0, v_res_ - 1);
                int id1 = v1 * h_res_ + u1;

                int u2 = clampValue((int)std::round(keypoints[m.trainIdx].pt.x), 0, h_res_ - 1);
                int v2 = clampValue((int)std::round(keypoints[m.trainIdx].pt.y), 0, v_res_ - 1);
                int id2 = v2 * h_res_ + u2;

                if (id1 >= 0 && id1 < (int)prev_px_.size() && id2 >= 0 && id2 < (int)pixel_x.size())
                {
                    if (prev_has_[id1] && has_point[id2])
                    {
                        P_all.emplace_back(prev_px_[id1], prev_py_[id1], prev_pz_[id1]);
                        Q_all.emplace_back(pixel_x[id2], pixel_y[id2], pixel_z[id2]);
                        matches_for_3d.push_back(m);
                    }
                }
            }

            // --- 如果没有足够的3D点，直接跳过3D-RANSAC与求解 ---
            if (P_all.size() < 3)
            {
                ROS_WARN("Frame %d: not enough 3D correspondences after 2D filtering: %zu", frame_idx_, P_all.size());
                std::ofstream ofs(output_dir_ + "/matches_3d_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".txt", std::ios::trunc);
                ofs.close();
            }
            else
            {
                // --- 3D-RANSAC ---
                auto t_r3_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
                std::vector<int> inlier_idx;
                if (enable_parallel_ransac3d_ && ransac3d_threads_ > 1)
                    inlier_idx = ransac3D_parallel(P_all, Q_all, ransac_3d_thresh_, ransac_3d_iters_, ransac3d_threads_);
                else
                    inlier_idx = ransac3D(P_all, Q_all, ransac_3d_thresh_, ransac_3d_iters_);
                auto t_r3_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
                t_ransac3d_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t_r3_end - t_r3_start).count();

                count_3d_inliers = inlier_idx.size();

                // build matches_3d_inliers list and save 3D pairs file
                std::vector<cv::DMatch> matches_3d_inliers;
                std::vector<cv::Point3f> P_in, Q_in;
                std::ofstream ofs3d(output_dir_ + "/matches_3d_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".txt", std::ios::trunc);
                for (int idx_in : inlier_idx)
                {
                    if (idx_in >= 0 && idx_in < (int)P_all.size())
                    {
                        P_in.push_back(P_all[(size_t)idx_in]);
                        Q_in.push_back(Q_all[(size_t)idx_in]);
                        matches_3d_inliers.push_back(matches_for_3d[(size_t)idx_in]);
                        ofs3d << P_all[(size_t)idx_in].x << " " << P_all[(size_t)idx_in].y << " " << P_all[(size_t)idx_in].z << " "
                              << Q_all[(size_t)idx_in].x << " " << Q_all[(size_t)idx_in].y << " " << Q_all[(size_t)idx_in].z << "\n";
                    }
                }
                ofs3d.close();
                ROS_INFO("Saved %zu 3D matches => %s", P_in.size(), (output_dir_ + "/matches_3d_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".txt").c_str());

                // --- save match visualization after 3D-RANSAC (只画 3D 内点) ---
                std::string vis3d_path = output_dir_ + "/match_3d_" + std::to_string(frame_idx_) + ".png";
                visualizeMatchesStacked(prev_img_, intensity_enh, prev_kp_, keypoints, matches_3d_inliers, vis3d_path);
                ROS_INFO("Saved 3D visualization: %s (inliers=%zu)", vis3d_path.c_str(), matches_3d_inliers.size());

                // --- 用 P_in,Q_in 做 SVD 求刚体变换并取逆得到车辆位姿（T_car） ---
                if (P_in.size() >= 3)
                {
                    cv::Mat Rfit, tfit;
                    if (estimateRigidSVD(P_in, Q_in, Rfit, tfit))
                    {
                        cv::Mat R_inv = Rfit.t();
                        cv::Mat t_inv = -R_inv * tfit;

                        // 计算欧拉角 (ZYX)
                        double roll = atan2(R_inv.at<double>(2, 1), R_inv.at<double>(2, 2));
                        double pitch = atan2(-R_inv.at<double>(2, 0), std::sqrt(R_inv.at<double>(2, 1) * R_inv.at<double>(2, 1) + R_inv.at<double>(2, 2) * R_inv.at<double>(2, 2)));
                        double yaw = atan2(R_inv.at<double>(1, 0), R_inv.at<double>(0, 0));

                        double tx = t_inv.at<double>(0), ty = t_inv.at<double>(1), tz = t_inv.at<double>(2);

                        // append to all_Tguess.txt
                        std::ofstream fout(output_dir_ + "/all_Tguess.txt", std::ios::app);
                        fout << frame_idx_ - 1 << " " << tx << " " << ty << " " << tz << " "
                             << (roll * 180.0 / M_PI) << " " << (pitch * 180.0 / M_PI) << " " << (yaw * 180.0 / M_PI) << "\n";
                        fout.close();

                        ROS_INFO("Tguess frame %d->%d: tx=%.3f ty=%.3f tz=%.3f roll=%.2f pitch=%.2f yaw=%.2f deg",
